--!nonstrict
-- INTERSTATE WEAVE - Roblox port. Single-player highway weaving; everything is simulated on the client.
-- Controls: W/S gas+brake, A/D steer, Q/E indicators (traffic makes room), R rewind 7 s, C camera, T rain, P pause.
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
local CATALOG = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("IWCatalog")) -- garage parts, prices and stat changes (shared with the server)
local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")

local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
local S = 3.571 -- studs per metre
local SEG_LEN, SEG_COUNT = 80, 20
local REWIND_S = 7

-- Web layout: our carriageway is x > 0, the oncoming one is mirrored at x < 0
local LANES = { 3.65, 7.35, 11.05, 14.75 }
local function laneX(i) return LANES[i] end
local OUTER_WALL, INNER_WALL = 20.08, 0.32
local ROAD_X1 = 19.6
local TERRAIN = { amp = 0, bend = 0 }
local function hillH(s) return math.sin(s * 0.0041 + 1.3) + math.sin(s * 0.0093 + 0.4) * 0.55 + math.sin(s * 0.021 + 2.1) * 0.18 end
local function hillX(s) return math.sin(s * 0.0023 + 0.7) + math.sin(s * 0.0061 + 2.9) * 0.45 end
-- ROAD GEOMETRY. The road is a chain of straight, rigid 80 m segments. Everything in the game (cars, camera, ramps, props) is placed
-- THROUGH the segment it is on, with the very same CFrame the road model itself is placed with, so nothing can disagree with the road
-- about where its surface is. (Before, cars followed a formula while the road pieces were rotated models: on hills with curves the two
-- drifted apart by up to half a metre, and the car sank into the road.)
local segCF
do
local segNodes, segFrames, nodeLo, nodeHi, geoAmp, geoBend = {}, {}, 0, 0, nil, nil
local function segDir(k) -- unit direction of segment k, from the hill and curve shape
	local f0 = k * SEG_LEN
	return Vector3.new((hillX(f0 + SEG_LEN) - hillX(f0)) * TERRAIN.bend, (hillH(f0 + SEG_LEN) - hillH(f0)) * TERRAIN.amp, -SEG_LEN).Unit
end
function segCF(k) -- centre of segment k's road surface, facing down the road (metres are turned into studs here)
	if geoAmp ~= TERRAIN.amp or geoBend ~= TERRAIN.bend then -- terrain changed: start the chain again
		geoAmp, geoBend = TERRAIN.amp, TERRAIN.bend
		segNodes = { [0] = Vector3.new(hillX(0) * geoBend, hillH(0) * geoAmp, 0) }; segFrames = {}; nodeLo, nodeHi = 0, 0
	end
	local c = segFrames[k]
	if c then return c end
	while nodeHi < k + 1 do segNodes[nodeHi + 1] = segNodes[nodeHi] + segDir(nodeHi) * SEG_LEN; nodeHi += 1 end
	while nodeLo > k do segNodes[nodeLo - 1] = segNodes[nodeLo] - segDir(nodeLo - 1) * SEG_LEN; nodeLo -= 1 end
	local a, b = segNodes[k], segNodes[k + 1]
	local mid = (a + b) / 2 * S
	c = CFrame.lookAt(mid, mid + (b - a))
	segFrames[k] = c
	return c
end
end
local function rnd(a, b) return a + math.random() * (b - a) end
local function clamp(v, a, b) return math.max(a, math.min(b, v)) end
-- a frame on the road at (x across, y above the surface, f along) that faces down the road
local function roadCF(x, y, f)
	local k = math.floor(f / SEG_LEN)
	return segCF(k) * CFrame.new(x * S, y * S, -(f - (k + 0.5) * SEG_LEN) * S)
end
-- road coordinates -> world studs
local function w2v(x, y, f)
	local k = math.floor(f / SEG_LEN)
	return segCF(k):PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(x * S, y * S, -(f - (k + 0.5) * SEG_LEN) * S))
end

---------------------------------------------------------------- default avatar out of the way
local function hideCharacter(ch)
	task.defer(function()
		local root = ch:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
		if root then root.Anchored = true; ch:PivotTo(CFrame.new(0, -2000, 0)) end
	end)
end
if player.Character then hideCharacter(player.Character) end
player.CharacterAdded:Connect(hideCharacter)
task.spawn(function()
	for _ = 1, 10 do
		if pcall(function() StarterGui:SetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Backpack, false) end) then break end
		task.wait(0.5)
	end
end)
camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable

---------------------------------------------------------------- part helpers
local world = Instance.new("Folder"); world.Name = "InterstateWorld"; world.Parent = Workspace

local function mk(parent, size, cf, color, mat, o)
	local p = Instance.new("Part")
	p.Anchored = true; p.CanCollide = false; p.CanTouch = false; p.CanQuery = false
	p.Size = size * S; p.CFrame = cf; p.Color = color
	p.Material = mat or Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	p.TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth; p.BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
	if o then
		if o.tr then p.Transparency = o.tr end
		if o.refl then p.Reflectance = o.refl end
		if o.shape then p.Shape = o.shape end
		if o.shadow == false then p.CastShadow = false end
	end
	p.Parent = parent
	return p
end

-- part welded to a car root (offsets in metres, optional X tilt in degrees)
local AMBER_OFF, AMBER_ON = Color3.fromRGB(70, 42, 8), Color3.fromRGB(255, 160, 30)
local buildCar, CAR_DIM
do
---------------------------------------------------------------- car builder: every car is assembled from a spec
-- Units are metres, the car faces -Z, y = 0 is the road. Each part is welded to an invisible root.
local GLASS = Color3.fromRGB(16, 24, 34)
local BLACK = Color3.fromRGB(14, 15, 17)
local TRIM = Color3.fromRGB(28, 29, 32)
local CHROME = Color3.fromRGB(196, 200, 206)
local RUBBER = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 22)
local LED = Color3.fromRGB(255, 250, 232)
local TAIL = Color3.fromRGB(255, 26, 18)
local ROT_Z = CFrame.Angles(0, math.pi / 2, 0) -- turns a cylinder's axis from X to Z

local function attach(car, p)
	p.Anchored = false; p.Massless = true; p.CanCollide = false; p.CanTouch = false; p.CanQuery = false
	local wc = Instance.new("WeldConstraint"); wc.Part0 = car.root; wc.Part1 = p; wc.Parent = p
	p.Parent = car.model
	return p
end
-- box/cylinder/ball part: size (sx, sy, sz) at (ox, oy, oz), optional extra rotation
local function cp(car, sx, sy, sz, ox, oy, oz, color, mat, o)
	local p = Instance.new("Part")
	p.Size = Vector3.new(math.max(sx, 0.02), math.max(sy, 0.02), math.max(sz, 0.02)) * S
	p.Color = color; p.Material = mat or Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	p.TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth; p.BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
	if o then
		if o.shape then p.Shape = o.shape end
		if o.refl then p.Reflectance = o.refl end
		if o.tr then p.Transparency = o.tr end
		if o.shadow == false then p.CastShadow = false end
	end
	if color == car.paint and (mat == nil or mat == Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic) and not (o and o.refl) then p.Reflectance = 0.14 end
	local rot = o and o.rot or CFrame.new()
	if o and o.rx then rot = rot * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(o.rx), 0, 0) end
	p.CFrame = car.root.CFrame * CFrame.new(ox * S, oy * S, oz * S) * rot
	if color == car.paint and car.hi then local pp = car.pp; if not pp then pp = {}; car.pp = pp end; pp[#pp + 1] = p end -- body paint, for finishes
	return attach(car, p)
end
-- sloped panel: tall end toward +Z (rear) unless flip
local function cw(car, sx, sy, sz, ox, oy, oz, color, mat, flip, o)
	local w = Instance.new("WedgePart")
	w.Size = Vector3.new(sx, sy, sz) * S; w.Color = color; w.Material = mat or Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
	if o and o.refl then w.Reflectance = o.refl elseif color == car.paint and mat == nil then w.Reflectance = 0.14 end
	w.CFrame = car.root.CFrame * CFrame.new(ox * S, oy * S, oz * S) * (flip and CFrame.Angles(0, math.pi, 0) or CFrame.new())
	if color == car.paint and car.hi then local pp = car.pp; if not pp then pp = {}; car.pp = pp end; pp[#pp + 1] = w end
	return attach(car, w)
end
-- box with rounded long edges: two crossed boxes plus four cylinders along the length
local function rbox(car, w, h, l, x, y, z, r, color, mat)
	if not car.hi then cp(car, w, h, l, x, y, z, color, mat); return end
	r = math.min(r, h / 2 - 0.01, w / 2 - 0.01)
	cp(car, w - 2 * r, h, l, x, y, z, color, mat)
	cp(car, w, h - 2 * r, l, x, y, z, color, mat)
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
		for _, sy in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
			cp(car, l, 2 * r, 2 * r, x + sx * (w / 2 - r), y + sy * (h / 2 - r), z, color, mat, { shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder, rot = ROT_Z })
		end
	end
end
local WHEEL_STYLE = { whl_five = { n = 5, th = 0.15 }, whl_mesh = { n = 18, th = 0.055 }, whl_turbo = { n = 0, cover = true }, whl_gold = { n = 5, th = 0.15, rim = Color3.fromRGB(205, 165, 40) } }
local function wheel(car, x, z, r, w, rimCol, caliper, spokes)
	local side = x > 0 and 1 or -1
	local ws = car.look and WHEEL_STYLE[car.look.wheels]
	if ws and ws.rim then rimCol = ws.rim end
	cp(car, w, r * 2, r * 2, x, r, z, RUBBER, Enum.Material.Rubber, { shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder })
	if not car.hi then
		cp(car, 0.04, r * 1.3, r * 1.3, x + side * (w / 2 + 0.006), r, z, rimCol, Enum.Material.Metal, { shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder, refl = 0.2 })
		return
	end
	cp(car, w * 0.5, r * 1.92, r * 1.92, x + side * w * 0.3, r, z, Color3.fromRGB(34, 34, 37), Enum.Material.Rubber, { shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder }) -- sidewall
	cp(car, 0.03, r * 1.34, r * 1.34, x + side * (w / 2 + 0.006), r, z, Color3.fromRGB(40, 41, 44), Enum.Material.Metal, { shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder }) -- disc behind spokes
	if caliper then cp(car, 0.05, r * 0.42, r * 0.3, x + side * (w / 2 - 0.02), r + r * 0.35, z + r * 0.25, caliper, Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic) end
	local n = ws and ws.n or spokes or 5
	local th = ws and ws.th or 0.15
	if ws and ws.cover then -- turbofan: a flat aero disc with dark blade slots
		cp(car, 0.04, r * 1.7, r * 1.7, x + side * (w / 2 + 0.018), r, z, Color3.fromRGB(150, 155, 162), Enum.Material.Metal, { shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder, refl = 0.3 })
		for k = 0, 4 do cp(car, 0.044, r * 1.5, r * 0.06, x + side * (w / 2 + 0.02), r, z, Color3.fromRGB(24, 25, 28), nil, { rot = CFrame.Angles(k * math.pi / 5, 0, 0) }) end
	end
	for k = 0, n - 1 do
		local a = k * 2 * math.pi / n
		cp(car, 0.035, r * 1.28, r * th, x + side * (w / 2 + 0.02), r, z, rimCol, Enum.Material.Metal, { rot = CFrame.Angles(a, 0, 0), refl = 0.3 })
	end
	cp(car, 0.04, r * 1.36, r * 1.36, x + side * (w / 2 + 0.012), r, z, rimCol, Enum.Material.Metal, { shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder, refl = 0.3, tr = 0.0 }).Transparency = 0.82 -- rim lip
	cp(car, 0.05, r * 0.36, r * 0.36, x + side * (w / 2 + 0.035), r, z, rimCol, Enum.Material.Metal, { shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder, refl = 0.35 }) -- hub
end
local function signals(car, x, yF, zF, yR, zR)
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
		local f = cp(car, 0.16, 0.07, 0.04, sx * x, yF, zF, AMBER_OFF, Enum.Material.Neon, { shadow = false })
		local r = cp(car, 0.16, 0.07, 0.04, sx * x, yR, zR, AMBER_OFF, Enum.Material.Neon, { shadow = false })
		if sx < 0 then car.sigL = { f, r } else car.sigR = { f, r } end
	end
end
local function plate(car, y, z)
	local s = z > 0 and 1 or -1
	cp(car, 0.52, 0.12, 0.02, 0, y, z + s * 0.005, Color3.fromRGB(236, 236, 230), Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic)
	cp(car, 0.36, 0.04, 0.022, 0, y, z + s * 0.008, Color3.fromRGB(40, 60, 120), Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic)
end

-- Cars rebuilt from the web version's own data. The browser game draws each car by extruding a side outline
-- (s forward, y up) across its width; here the very same outlines, wheelbases, light positions, wings, scoops,
-- fins and stripes are rebuilt from blocks and wedges, so the shapes line up with the web cars.
-- Web forward distance s becomes -Z in the car's space (the car faces -Z).
local ACCENT_DEF = Color3.fromRGB(17, 18, 20)
local RIM_DEF = Color3.fromRGB(169, 175, 181)
local CALIPER = Color3.fromRGB(179, 18, 26)
local PLATE_C = Color3.fromRGB(230, 228, 218)
local GLASS_O = { refl = 0.3 }
local HEAD_C = Color3.fromRGB(240, 244, 246)
local TAIL_C = Color3.fromRGB(214, 26, 18)

-- vertical extent of a closed outline at one s
local function envelope(poly, z)
	local lo, hi = math.huge, -math.huge
	local n = #poly
	for i = 1, n do
		local a, b = poly[i], poly[i % n + 1]
		local za, zb = a[1], b[1]
		if za ~= zb and ((za <= z and z <= zb) or (zb <= z and z <= za)) then
			local y = a[2] + (b[2] - a[2]) * (z - za) / (zb - za)
			if y < lo then lo = y end
			if y > hi then hi = y end
		end
	end
	return lo, hi
end
-- a block up to the lower of two heights with a wedge on top for the slope between them (front = larger s)
local function topped(car, width, x, y0, yF, yR, zF, zR, color, mat, o)
	local len, zc = zF - zR, -(zF + zR) / 2
	local ylow = math.min(yF, yR)
	if ylow - y0 > 0.01 then cp(car, width, ylow - y0, len, x, (y0 + ylow) / 2, zc, color, mat, o) end
	local d = math.abs(yF - yR)
	if d > 0.01 then cw(car, width, d, len, x, ylow + d / 2, zc, color, mat, yF > yR, o) end
end
-- fill a closed side outline across a width, a slab at a time, with a small bevel at the top edges
local function slabs(car, poly, width, color, mat, ox, o)
	local bev = 0.06
	local zs = {}
	for _, p in ipairs(poly) do zs[#zs + 1] = p[1] end
	table.sort(zs, function(a, b) return a > b end)
	local u = {}
	for _, z in ipairs(zs) do if #u == 0 or u[#u] - z > 0.015 then u[#u + 1] = z end end
	for i = 1, #u - 1 do
		local za, zb = u[i], u[i + 1]
		local loA, hiA = envelope(poly, za - 0.0005)
		local loB, hiB = envelope(poly, zb + 0.0005)
		if loA ~= math.huge and loB ~= math.huge then
			local ybot, ylow = math.min(loA, loB), math.min(hiA, hiB)
			local lowTop = ylow - bev
			if lowTop - ybot > 0.02 then
				topped(car, width, ox or 0, ybot, lowTop, lowTop, za, zb, color, mat, o)
				topped(car, width - 2 * bev, ox or 0, lowTop, hiA, hiB, za, zb, color, mat, o)
			else
				topped(car, width - 2 * bev, ox or 0, ybot, hiA, hiB, za, zb, color, mat, o)
			end
		end
	end
end
-- dark wheel wells so each wheel sits in an arch like the web cars
local function archDiscs(car, poly, W, wheels, wr, clear)
	for _, w in ipairs(wheels) do
		local _, hi = envelope(poly, w)
		local r = math.min(wr + 0.12, (hi or 1) - clear - 0.03)
		if r > 0.15 then cp(car, W + 0.03, 2 * r, 2 * r, 0, clear, -w, BLACK, nil, { shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder, shadow = false }) end
	end
end
-- helpers for placing web-style parts: every call takes the web's (w, h, l, x, y, s)
local function mkX(car, accentCol, ledCol)
	local X = {}
	local function maker(color, mat, o) return function(w, h, l, x, y, s) return cp(car, w, h, l, x, y, -s, color, mat, o) end end
	X.paint = maker(car.paint)
	X.trim = maker(TRIM)
	X.chrome = maker(CHROME, Enum.Material.Metal, { refl = 0.35 })
	X.accent = maker(accentCol, nil, { refl = 0.1 })
	X.led = maker(ledCol, Enum.Material.Neon, { shadow = false })
	X.glass = maker(GLASS, nil, GLASS_O)
	local kinds = { paint = car.paint, trim = TRIM, accent = accentCol, glass = GLASS }
	X.poly = function(poly, width, kind, ox) slabs(car, poly, width, kinds[kind], nil, ox, kind == "glass" and GLASS_O or nil) end
	X.cyl = function(r, len, x, y, s, color) return cp(car, len, 2 * r, 2 * r, x, y, -s, color or CHROME, Enum.Material.Metal, { shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder, rot = ROT_Z }) end
	return X
end

-- the add-ons each web car carries (wings, scoops, stripes, fins, sills ...)
local EXTRA = {
	sport = function(X, s) X.paint(1.5, 0.04, 0.22, 0, 1.0, -2.15); X.accent(0.3, 0.012, 1.0, 0, 1.31, -0.4) end,
	muscle = function(X, s)
		X.poly({ { 1.95, 0.94 }, { 1.85, 1.1 }, { 1.05, 1.1 }, { 0.95, 0.97 } }, 0.62, "paint") -- hood scoop
		X.trim(0.5, 0.08, 0.04, 0, 1.03, 1.93)
		for _, sx in ipairs({ -0.17, 0.17 }) do
			X.accent(0.2, 0.012, 0.95, sx, 1.355, -0.32); X.accent(0.2, 0.012, 0.75, sx, 1.115, 1.45); X.accent(0.2, 0.012, 0.75, sx, 1.075, -2.0)
		end
		X.paint(1.7, 0.05, 0.18, 0, 1.08, -2.36)
		X.led(s.W - 0.4, 0.05, 0.04, 0, 0.76, -2.53)
		for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do X.cyl(0.06, 0.5, sx * 0.55, 0.32, -2.5) end
	end,
	wedge = function(X, s)
		local W = s.W
		X.trim(W * 0.96, 0.05, 0.42, 0, 1.3, -2.05) -- rear wing
		for _, sx in ipairs({ -0.55, 0.55 }) do X.trim(0.05, 0.36, 0.14, sx, 1.1, -2.0) end
		for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
			X.accent(0.03, 0.3, 0.46, sx * W * 0.48, 1.3, -2.05)
			X.trim(0.02, 0.26, 0.95, sx * (W / 2 + 0.03), 0.6, -0.75)
			X.led(0.5, 0.035, 0.05, sx * (W / 2 - 0.36), 0.5, 2.28)
		end
		X.trim(W * 0.95, 0.04, 0.32, 0, 0.2, 2.2) -- splitter
		for i = -2, 2 do X.trim(0.03, 0.18, 0.4, i * 0.3, 0.3, -2.2) end -- diffuser
		X.led(W - 0.3, 0.05, 0.04, 0, 0.84, -2.36)
		X.accent(0.36, 0.012, 2.2, 0, 0.83, 1.3)
	end,
	hyper = function(X, s)
		local W = s.W
		X.poly({ { -0.7, 1.08 }, { -2.2, 1.22 }, { -2.32, 0.8 }, { -1.2, 0.76 } }, 0.05, "accent") -- shark fin
		X.trim(W, 0.05, 0.48, 0, 1.28, -2.25)
		for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
			X.accent(0.03, 0.42, 0.6, sx * W / 2, 1.18, -2.25)
			X.poly({ { 2.05, 0.4 }, { 1.95, 0.8 }, { 1.0, 0.82 }, { 0.9, 0.45 } }, 0.44, "paint", sx * (W / 2 - 0.22)) -- front fender pods
			X.poly({ { -0.9, 0.5 }, { -1.0, 0.88 }, { -2.1, 0.86 }, { -2.2, 0.5 } }, 0.44, "paint", sx * (W / 2 - 0.22)) -- rear haunches
			X.trim(0.02, 0.3, 0.7, sx * (W / 2 + 0.01), 0.52, -0.3)
		end
		X.led(W - 0.5, 0.04, 0.04, 0, 0.43, 2.4); X.led(W - 0.3, 0.06, 0.04, 0, 0.72, -2.46)
		X.trim(W * 0.95, 0.04, 0.4, 0, 0.18, 2.25)
	end,
	ev = function(X, s)
		local W = s.W
		X.led(W - 0.25, 0.035, 0.04, 0, 0.64, 2.41); X.led(W - 0.2, 0.05, 0.04, 0, 0.82, -2.45)
		for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do X.accent(0.012, 0.05, 3.2, sx * (W / 2 + 0.065), 0.42, 0.05) end -- light-blade sills
		X.trim(W * 0.9, 0.12, 0.2, 0, 0.27, -2.38)
	end,
	pickup = function(X, s) X.trim(s.W - 0.26, 0.04, 2.25, 0, 1.31, -1.6); X.trim(s.W - 0.26, 0.05, 0.08, 0, 1.31, -0.42) end,
	van = function(X, s)
		for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do X.glass(0.02, 0.62, 0.8, sx * (s.W * 0.495 + 0.01), 1.9, 0.82) end
		X.trim(0.02, 2.0, 0.02, 0, 1.6, -3.02)
	end,
}

EXTRA.pip = function(X, s)
	X.paint(1.1, 0.04, 0.14, 0, 1.46, -1.62); X.trim(s.W * 0.9, 0.08, 0.06, 0, 0.34, 1.77)
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do X.accent(0.1, 0.1, 0.3, sx * (s.W / 2 - 0.12), 0.58, 1.7) end
end
EXTRA.pipr = function(X, s)
	X.paint(1.45, 0.05, 0.34, 0, 1.5, -1.8); X.trim(s.W * 0.9, 0.05, 0.2, 0, 0.3, 1.95); X.accent(0.3, 0.012, 1.0, 0, 0.9, 1.3)
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do X.trim(0.04, 0.2, 0.3, sx * 0.7, 1.4, -1.72) end
	X.cyl(0.05, 0.4, 0.5, 0.3, -1.95)
end
EXTRA.meridian = function(X, s)
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do X.chrome(0.02, 0.03, 3.0, sx * (s.W / 2 + 0.01), 0.95, 0) end
	X.paint(1.3, 0.04, 0.2, 0, 0.97, -2.15); X.led(s.W - 0.5, 0.04, 0.04, 0, 0.84, -2.4)
end
EXTRA.vanline = function(X, s)
	for k = -1, 1 do X.trim(s.W - 0.3, 0.04, 0.05, 0, 2.16, k * 0.9) end
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
		X.glass(0.02, 0.5, 1.2, sx * (s.W * 0.48 + 0.01), 1.7, -0.2); X.trim(0.02, 0.9, 0.02, sx * (s.W * 0.495 + 0.01), 1.4, 0.6); X.trim(0.04, 0.04, 2.9, sx * (s.W * 0.4), 2.16, -0.2)
	end
	X.trim(0.9, 0.18, 0.04, 0, 0.8, 2.3)
end
EXTRA.ridgeback = function(X, s)
	for _, z in ipairs({ -1.6, -0.7, 0.2 }) do X.trim(s.W - 0.4, 0.05, 0.05, 0, 1.9, z) end
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do X.trim(0.05, 0.06, 2.2, sx * (s.W / 2 - 0.2), 1.9, -0.7); X.trim(0.06, 0.5, 0.06, sx * 0.5, 0.8, 2.3) end
	X.trim(1.1, 0.07, 0.07, 0, 0.95, 2.32); X.cyl(0.4, 0.22, 0, 0.9, -2.3, RUBBER)
end
EXTRA.hauler = function(X, s)
	X.trim(1.2, 0.08, 0.12, 0, 1.99, 0.1)
	for i = -2, 2 do X.led(0.12, 0.06, 0.04, i * 0.22, 1.99, 0.18) end
	X.trim(s.W - 0.26, 0.04, 2.2, 0, 1.35, -1.65); X.trim(1.2, 0.08, 0.08, 0, 0.95, 2.76)
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do X.trim(0.1, 0.04, 2.4, sx * (s.W / 2), 0.5, 0.2) end
end
EXTRA.vantars = function(X, s)
	X.paint(1.4, 0.04, 0.24, 0, 0.98, -2.2)
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do X.accent(0.12, 0.012, 2.4, sx * 0.12, 0.95, 1.1); X.accent(0.012, 0.05, 3.0, sx * (s.W / 2 + 0.008), 0.5, -0.2) end
	X.led(s.W - 0.4, 0.04, 0.04, 0, 0.86, -2.4)
end
EXTRA.gt500 = function(X, s)
	X.poly({ { 1.6, 0.93 }, { 1.45, 1.15 }, { 0.7, 1.15 }, { 0.6, 0.98 } }, 0.7, "paint")
	X.paint(1.8, 0.05, 0.2, 0, 1.0, -2.45)
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -0.16, 0.16 }) do X.accent(0.2, 0.012, 1.2, sx, 0.945, 2.0); X.accent(0.2, 0.012, 0.95, sx, 1.305, -0.4) end
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do X.cyl(0.06, 0.5, sx * 0.55, 0.32, -2.5); X.cyl(0.04, 1.0, sx * (s.W / 2 - 0.05), 0.26, 0.4) end
end
EXTRA.kestrels = function(X, s)
	local W = s.W
	X.poly({ { 0.2, 1.05 }, { 0.1, 1.22 }, { -0.4, 1.22 }, { -0.5, 1.06 } }, 0.3, "accent")
	X.trim(W * 0.96, 0.05, 0.42, 0, 1.22, -2.0); X.trim(W * 0.95, 0.04, 0.32, 0, 0.18, 2.15)
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do X.trim(0.03, 0.2, 0.5, sx * (W / 2 - 0.1), 0.34, 2.0); X.trim(0.03, 0.22, 0.9, sx * (W / 2 + 0.02), 0.55, -0.7); X.trim(0.05, 0.3, 0.14, sx * 0.55, 1.05, -1.95) end
	X.led(W - 0.3, 0.05, 0.04, 0, 0.8, -2.2)
end
EXTRA.phantomx = function(X, s)
	local W = s.W
	X.led(W - 0.3, 0.03, 0.04, 0, 0.62, 2.55); X.led(W - 0.25, 0.04, 0.04, 0, 0.82, -2.52)
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do X.accent(0.012, 0.05, 3.4, sx * (W / 2 + 0.065), 0.4, 0.05) end
	X.trim(W * 0.9, 0.12, 0.2, 0, 0.27, -2.5)
end
EXTRA.apexr = function(X, s)
	local W = s.W
	X.trim(W, 0.05, 0.55, 0, 1.32, -2.4)
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
		X.accent(0.03, 0.5, 0.7, sx * W / 2, 1.2, -2.4); X.trim(W * 0.25, 0.03, 0.3, sx * 0.45, 0.28, 2.35)
		X.poly({ { -0.5, 0.95 }, { -2.3, 1.1 }, { -2.35, 0.7 }, { -0.9, 0.66 } }, 0.05, "accent", sx * 0.25)
	end
	X.led(W - 0.4, 0.04, 0.04, 0, 0.62, -2.47)
end
EXTRA.or2 = function(X, s)
	local W = s.W
	X.poly({ { 0.2, 1.0 }, { 0.1, 1.14 }, { -0.4, 1.14 }, { -0.5, 1.02 } }, 0.26, "accent")
	X.poly({ { -0.5, 1.0 }, { -2.4, 1.2 }, { -2.45, 0.7 }, { -0.9, 0.7 } }, 0.05, "accent")
	X.trim(W, 0.05, 0.5, 0, 1.25, -2.5)
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do X.accent(0.03, 0.4, 0.6, sx * W / 2, 1.15, -2.5) end
	X.trim(W * 0.95, 0.04, 0.4, 0, 0.14, 2.3)
end
EXTRA.nova = function(X, s)
	local W = s.W
	X.trim(W, 0.05, 0.5, 0, 0.18, 2.35); X.led(W - 0.4, 0.05, 0.04, 0, 0.7, -2.4)
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do X.accent(0.02, 0.35, 1.1, sx * (W / 2 - 0.02), 0.7, 0.2); X.trim(0.03, 0.2, 0.7, sx * (W / 2 + 0.03), 0.4, -0.9) end
	for i = -2, 2 do X.trim(0.03, 0.2, 0.4, i * 0.3, 0.26, -2.3) end
end
EXTRA.specter = function(X, s)
	local W = s.W
	X.accent(W * 0.8, 0.03, 0.4, 0, 0.24, 2.45); X.trim(0.04, 0.4, 1.2, 0, 1.0, -1.9)
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do X.trim(0.03, 0.3, 1.4, sx * (W / 2 + 0.03), 0.55, -0.2); X.led(0.06, 0.03, 3.5, sx * (W / 2 - 0.2), 0.2, 0) end
	X.led(W - 0.5, 0.03, 0.04, 0, 0.6, -2.54)
end
EXTRA.eclipse = function(X, s)
	local W = s.W
	X.trim(W, 0.05, 0.5, 0, 1.3, -2.5); X.trim(W * 0.9, 0.04, 0.35, 0, 1.45, -2.55)
	X.poly({ { -0.4, 0.98 }, { -2.45, 1.4 }, { -2.5, 0.75 }, { -1.2, 0.7 } }, 0.04, "accent")
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do X.accent(0.02, 0.1, 2.6, sx * (W / 2 + 0.01), 0.45, 0); X.accent(0.03, 0.55, 0.7, sx * W / 2, 1.3, -2.5) end
	X.led(W - 0.4, 0.04, 0.04, 0, 0.62, -2.55); X.trim(W * 0.95, 0.04, 0.4, 0, 0.14, 2.45)
end

local function buildWeb(car, c, name, cfg)
	cfg = cfg or {}
	local s = WSPEC[name]
	car.spec = s
	local W = s.W
	local X = mkX(car, cfg.accent or ACCENT_DEF, cfg.led or Color3.fromRGB(255, 42, 26))
	slabs(car, s.body, W, car.paint)
	for _, sh in ipairs(s.shells) do
		if sh.mat == "glass" then slabs(car, sh.pts, W * sh.w, GLASS, nil, nil, GLASS_O) else slabs(car, sh.pts, W * sh.w, car.paint) end
	end
	archDiscs(car, s.body, W, s.wheels, s.wr, s.clear)
	local fS, rS = s.headS + 0.06, s.tailS - 0.06
	car.sigL, car.sigR = {}, {}
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
		cp(car, 0.44, 0.12, 0.1, sx * (W / 2 - 0.33), s.headY, -(fS - 0.03), HEAD_C, Enum.Material.Neon, { shadow = false })
		cp(car, 0.48, 0.13, 0.1, sx * (W / 2 - 0.3), s.tailY, -(rS + 0.03), TAIL_C, Enum.Material.Neon, { shadow = false })
		local list = sx < 0 and car.sigL or car.sigR
		list[#list + 1] = cp(car, 0.12, 0.1, 0.1, sx * (W / 2 - 0.07), s.tailY, -(rS + 0.025), AMBER_OFF, Enum.Material.Neon, { shadow = false })
		list[#list + 1] = cp(car, 0.12, 0.08, 0.1, sx * (W / 2 - 0.1), s.headY - 0.1, -(fS - 0.035), AMBER_OFF, Enum.Material.Neon, { shadow = false })
		X.paint(0.1, 0.12, 0.22, sx * (W / 2 + 0.07), s.mirror[2], s.mirror[1])
	end
	X.trim(W * 0.42, 0.16, 0.08, 0, s.headY - 0.17, fS - 0.02)
	X.trim(W * 0.86, 0.13, 0.14, 0, s.clear + 0.07, s.body[1][1] + 0.03)
	X.trim(W * 0.9, 0.14, 0.14, 0, s.clear + 0.07, s.body[#s.body][1] - 0.03)
	cp(car, 0.52, 0.12, 0.03, 0, s.tailY - 0.32, -(rS - 0.02), PLATE_C)
	if EXTRA[name] then EXTRA[name](X, s) end
	local rim = cfg.rim or RIM_DEF
	for _, w in ipairs(s.wheels) do
		for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
			wheel(car, sx * (W / 2 - s.tireW / 2 - 0.02), -w, s.wr, s.tireW, rim, CALIPER, 5)
		end
	end
	if cfg.glow then
		cp(car, W * 1.9, 0.03, s.L * 1.25, 0, 0.02, 0, cfg.glow, Enum.Material.Neon, { shape = Enum.PartType.Ball, tr = 0.82, shadow = false })
	end
end

local function cfg(rim, accent, led, glow) return { rim = Color3.fromHex(rim), accent = Color3.fromHex(accent), led = Color3.fromHex(led), glow = glow and Color3.fromHex(glow) or nil } end
local CFG = {
	vanta = cfg("a9afb5", "101113", "ff2a1a"),
	strada = cfg("18191b", "f2f2ee", "ff2a1a", "ff5a1a"),
	kestrel = cfg("202124", "f5b524", "ffffff", "22d3ff"),
	phantom = cfg("d2d6da", "7ff0ff", "7ff0ff", "8a4dff"),
	apex = cfg("c9a227", "ff2bd6", "ff2bd6", "ff2bd6"),
	or1 = cfg("2a2d31", "111214", "ff2a1a"),
}
local BUILD = {}
BUILD.vanta = function(car, c) buildWeb(car, c, "sport", CFG.vanta) end
BUILD.strada = function(car, c) buildWeb(car, c, "muscle", CFG.strada) end
BUILD.kestrel = function(car, c) buildWeb(car, c, "wedge", CFG.kestrel) end
BUILD.apex = function(car, c) buildWeb(car, c, "hyper", CFG.apex) end
BUILD.phantom = function(car, c) buildWeb(car, c, "ev", CFG.phantom) end
BUILD.sedan = function(car, c) buildWeb(car, c, "sedan") end
BUILD.hatch = function(car, c) buildWeb(car, c, "hatch") end
BUILD.suv = function(car, c) buildWeb(car, c, "suv") end
BUILD.pickup = function(car, c) buildWeb(car, c, "pickup") end
BUILD.van = function(car, c) buildWeb(car, c, "van") end
BUILD.police = function(car, c)
	buildWeb(car, c, "sedan")
	local W = WSPEC.sedan.W
	local white = Color3.fromRGB(241, 241, 238)
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do cp(car, 0.03, 0.4, 2.3, sx * (W / 2 + 0.055), 0.64, -0.1, white, nil, { refl = 0.12 }) end
	cp(car, 1.25, 0.06, 0.34, 0, 1.5, 0.4, TRIM)
	car.red = cp(car, 0.56, 0.11, 0.28, -0.32, 1.58, 0.4, Color3.fromRGB(64, 0, 0), Enum.Material.Neon, { shadow = false })
	car.blue = cp(car, 0.56, 0.11, 0.28, 0.32, 1.58, 0.4, Color3.fromRGB(0, 10, 64), Enum.Material.Neon, { shadow = false })
	cp(car, 1.25, 0.32, 0.1, 0, 0.55, -2.55, TRIM) -- push bar
	local rl = Instance.new("PointLight"); rl.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 30, 30); rl.Range = 16; rl.Brightness = 0; rl.Parent = car.red; car.redL = rl
	local bl = Instance.new("PointLight"); bl.Color = Color3.fromRGB(40, 80, 255); bl.Range = 16; bl.Brightness = 0; bl.Parent = car.blue; car.blueL = bl
end

-- the tractor-trailer, from the web version's semi model
local LOGOS = { { "NORTHLINE", Color3.fromRGB(23, 58, 107) }, { "CASCADE FREIGHT", Color3.fromRGB(13, 107, 79) }, { "BLUE RIDGE LOGISTICS", Color3.fromRGB(31, 78, 140) }, { "IRONWOOD", Color3.fromRGB(122, 27, 27) } }
BUILD.semi = function(car, c)
	local W, wr = 2.5, 0.52
	local X = mkX(car, ACCENT_DEF, TAIL_C)
	local cab = { { 9.45, 0.6 }, { 9.55, 0.95 }, { 9.5, 1.6 }, { 9.25, 1.82 }, { 7.9, 1.98 }, { 4.9, 1.98 }, { 4.9, 0.6 } }
	slabs(car, cab, W, car.paint)
	X.poly({ { 7.95, 1.96 }, { 7.45, 3.0 }, { 6.95, 3.0 }, { 6.95, 1.96 } }, W * 0.9, "glass")
	X.poly({ { 6.98, 1.96 }, { 6.98, 3.02 }, { 4.9, 3.05 }, { 4.9, 1.96 } }, W * 0.98, "paint")
	X.poly({ { 7.4, 2.98 }, { 6.6, 3.72 }, { 4.95, 3.82 }, { 4.95, 2.98 } }, W * 0.95, "paint")
	archDiscs(car, cab, W, { 8.3 }, wr, 0.6)
	car.sigL, car.sigR = {}, {}
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
		X.glass(0.02, 0.66, 0.85, sx * (W * 0.49 + 0.012), 2.52, 6.45)
		cp(car, 0.42, 0.2, 0.08, sx * 0.92, 1.12, -9.55, HEAD_C, Enum.Material.Neon, { shadow = false })
		X.cyl(0.3, 1.3, sx * 1.0, 0.85, 4.1) -- fuel tanks
		cp(car, 2.6, 0.16, 0.16, sx * 1.08, 3.0, -4.85, CHROME, Enum.Material.Metal, { shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder, rot = CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi / 2) }) -- exhaust stacks
		X.paint(0.1, 0.3, 0.12, sx * (W / 2 + 0.25), 2.55, 7.3)
		cp(car, 0.3, 0.16, 0.06, sx * 1.0, 1.42, 9.53, TAIL_C, Enum.Material.Neon, { shadow = false })
		local list = sx < 0 and car.sigL or car.sigR
		list[#list + 1] = cp(car, 0.16, 0.16, 0.06, sx * 1.22, 1.42, 9.53, AMBER_OFF, Enum.Material.Neon, { shadow = false })
		list[#list + 1] = cp(car, 0.2, 0.12, 0.08, sx * 1.22, 1.12, -9.53, AMBER_OFF, Enum.Material.Neon, { shadow = false })
		X.trim(0.5, 0.06, 1.3, sx * 1.0, 1.17, 2.95)
	end
	X.chrome(1.3, 0.95, 0.06, 0, 1.2, 9.56)
	X.chrome(2.5, 0.3, 0.26, 0, 0.62, 9.52)
	X.trim(1.0, 0.3, 6.9, 0, 0.88, 1.45)
	-- the trailer
	cp(car, 2.6, 2.75, 13.9, 0, 2.68, 2.55, Color3.fromRGB(242, 242, 239), nil, { refl = 0.05 })
	X.trim(2.6, 0.16, 13.9, 0, 1.24, -2.55)
	X.trim(0.05, 2.6, 0.03, 0, 2.68, -9.52); X.trim(2.4, 0.12, 0.1, 0, 0.62, -9.3)
	X.trim(0.12, 0.6, 0.1, -0.9, 0.9, -9.3); X.trim(0.12, 0.6, 0.1, 0.9, 0.9, -9.3)
	X.trim(0.12, 1.0, 0.12, -0.9, 0.7, 2.0); X.trim(0.12, 1.0, 0.12, 0.9, 0.7, 2.0)
	cp(car, 0.5, 0.15, 0.03, 0, 0.9, 9.54, PLATE_C)
	-- company name on both sides
	local logo = LOGOS[math.random(1, #LOGOS)]
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
		local p = cp(car, 0.02, 2.5, 13.4, sx * 1.305, 2.7, 2.55, Color3.fromRGB(244, 244, 241))
		local g = Instance.new("SurfaceGui"); g.Face = sx > 0 and Enum.NormalId.Right or Enum.NormalId.Left
		g.SizingMode = Enum.SurfaceGuiSizingMode.FixedSize; g.CanvasSize = Vector2.new(1340, 250); g.Parent = p
		local t = Instance.new("TextLabel"); t.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.9, 0.55); t.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.04, 0.12)
		t.BackgroundTransparency = 1; t.Text = logo[1]; t.TextColor3 = logo[2]; t.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack; t.TextScaled = true
		t.TextXAlignment = sx > 0 and Enum.TextXAlignment.Left or Enum.TextXAlignment.Right; t.Parent = g
		local bar = Instance.new("Frame"); bar.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0.08); bar.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.8); bar.BackgroundColor3 = logo[2]; bar.BorderSizePixel = 0; bar.Parent = g
	end
	for _, ax in ipairs({ { 8.3, 0.3 }, { 3.6, 0.55 }, { 2.3, 0.55 }, { -6.4, 0.55 }, { -7.7, 0.55 } }) do
		for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do wheel(car, sx * (W / 2 - ax[2] / 2 - 0.02), -ax[1], wr, ax[2], RIM_DEF, nil, 5) end
	end
end

-- the OR-1 hypercar: built from the same station table the web model sculpts its body from
local OR1_KEY = { -- s, half-width, floor, widest-point height, fender-crown height, crown position, centreline height
	{ 2.33, 0.74, 0.130, 0.215, 0.300, 0.74, 0.290 }, { 2.26, 0.85, 0.118, 0.235, 0.355, 0.76, 0.325 }, { 2.10, 0.925, 0.112, 0.270, 0.450, 0.79, 0.380 }, { 1.85, 0.985, 0.110, 0.335, 0.610, 0.80, 0.455 },
	{ 1.36, 1.015, 0.110, 0.430, 0.780, 0.80, 0.575 }, { 0.92, 1.010, 0.110, 0.450, 0.760, 0.80, 0.660 }, { 0.45, 0.990, 0.110, 0.455, 0.705, 0.81, 0.715 }, { -0.30, 0.985, 0.110, 0.465, 0.700, 0.82, 0.750 },
	{ -0.90, 1.005, 0.110, 0.495, 0.790, 0.82, 0.815 }, { -1.44, 1.020, 0.120, 0.530, 0.905, 0.80, 0.850 }, { -1.95, 0.995, 0.180, 0.565, 0.895, 0.80, 0.845 }, { -2.20, 0.955, 0.255, 0.590, 0.855, 0.80, 0.822 },
	{ -2.30, 0.925, 0.300, 0.600, 0.822, 0.80, 0.800 },
}
local OR1_CAN = { { 1.02, 0.46, 0.56 }, { 0.80, 0.585, 0.745 }, { 0.50, 0.675, 0.925 }, { 0.15, 0.725, 1.085 }, { -0.25, 0.735, 1.165 }, { -0.70, 0.705, 1.150 }, { -1.10, 0.620, 1.060 }, { -1.50, 0.500, 0.955 }, { -1.90, 0.360, 0.850 } }
local function buildOr1(car, c, cf)
	local W, wr, wheels = 2.04, 0.36, { 1.36, -1.44 }
	do -- outline for the garage kits and decals
		local top, bot, can = {}, {}, {}
		for _, a in ipairs(OR1_KEY) do top[#top + 1] = { a[1], a[4] }; table.insert(bot, 1, { a[1], a[3] }) end
		for _, a in ipairs(OR1_CAN) do can[#can + 1] = { a[1], a[3] } end
		for i = #OR1_CAN, 1, -1 do can[#can + 1] = { OR1_CAN[i][1], 0.5 } end
		local body = {}
		for _, p in ipairs(top) do body[#body + 1] = p end
		for _, p in ipairs(bot) do body[#body + 1] = p end
		car.spec = { W = W, L = 4.66, headS = 2.33, tailS = -2.3, tailY = 0.66, clear = 0.1, wheels = wheels, wr = wr, tireW = 0.31, body = body, shells = { { pts = can, mat = "glass" } } }
	end
	local X = mkX(car, cf.accent, cf.led)
	local paint = car.paint
	for i = 1, #OR1_KEY - 1 do
		local a, b = OR1_KEY[i], OR1_KEY[i + 1]
		local za, zb = a[1], b[1]
		local function v(k) return a[k], b[k] end
		local hw = (a[2] + b[2]) / 2
		local kx = (a[6] + b[6]) / 2
		local ybF, ybR = v(3)
		local ymF, ymR = v(4)
		local yfF, yfR = v(5)
		local ycF, ycR = v(7)
		local floorY = math.min(ybF, ybR)
		topped(car, hw * 2 * 0.99, 0, floorY, ymF, ymR, za, zb, paint)
		local shF = math.max(ymF + 0.02, math.min(ymF + (yfF - ymF) * 0.55, ycF))
		local shR = math.max(ymR + 0.02, math.min(ymR + (yfR - ymR) * 0.55, ycR))
		topped(car, hw * 2 * (kx + (1 - kx) * 0.5), 0, math.min(ymF, ymR), shF, shR, za, zb, paint)
		local sBase = math.min(shF, shR)
		if math.min(ycF, ycR) > sBase then topped(car, hw * 2 * kx * 0.82, 0, sBase, ycF, ycR, za, zb, paint) end
		if math.max(yfF, yfR) > sBase + 0.02 then
			for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do topped(car, 0.34, sx * hw * kx * 0.9, sBase, math.max(yfF, sBase), math.max(yfR, sBase), za, zb, paint) end
		end
	end
	-- glass canopy with a painted roof and cant rails
	for i = 1, #OR1_CAN - 1 do
		local a, b = OR1_CAN[i], OR1_CAN[i + 1]
		local sc = (a[1] + b[1]) / 2
		local hw = (a[2] + b[2]) / 2 * 0.86
		if sc > -0.92 then
			topped(car, hw * 2, 0, 0.5, a[3], b[3], a[1], b[1], GLASS, nil, GLASS_O)
			if sc < -0.06 then topped(car, hw * 0.9, 0, math.min(a[3], b[3]), a[3] + 0.02, b[3] + 0.02, a[1], b[1], paint) end
		else
			topped(car, hw * 2, 0, 0.5, a[3], b[3], a[1], b[1], paint)
			if sc > -1.72 then topped(car, hw * 0.8, 0, math.min(a[3], b[3]), a[3] + 0.015, b[3] + 0.015, a[1], b[1], GLASS, nil, GLASS_O) end
		end
	end
	for _, w in ipairs(wheels) do
		cp(car, W + 0.03, 0.82, 0.82, 0, w == wheels[1] and 0.355 or 0.36, -w, BLACK, nil, { shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder, shadow = false })
	end
	-- aero: splitter, canards, mirrors, rear wing, diffuser
	X.accent(1.96, 0.025, 0.55, 0, 0.08, 2.135)
	slabs(car, { { -1.92, 1.065 }, { -2.0, 1.092 }, { -2.12, 1.105 }, { -2.24, 1.1 }, { -2.33, 1.085 }, { -2.24, 1.068 }, { -2.1, 1.058 }, { -1.98, 1.056 } }, 1.78, cf.accent, nil, nil, { refl = 0.1 })
	X.led(0.5, 0.012, 0.008, 0, 1.082, -2.334)
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
		X.accent(0.05, 0.02, 1.96, sx * 1.0, 0.1, -0.03)
		slabs(car, { { -1.72, 0.84 }, { -2.02, 0.83 }, { -2.2, 1.05 }, { -2.06, 1.07 } }, 0.04, cf.accent, nil, sx * 0.3225)
		X.accent(0.015, 0.2, 0.46, sx * 0.8975, 1.05, -2.1)
		X.paint(0.12, 0.07, 0.22, sx * 1.0, 0.84, 0.36)
		X.accent(0.022, 0.12, 0.1, sx * 0.8, 0.31, 2.1)
	end
	X.poly({ { -1.7, 0.105 }, { -2.36, 0.285 }, { -2.36, 0.305 }, { -1.7, 0.125 } }, 1.8, "accent")
	-- lights
	car.sigL, car.sigR = {}, {}
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
		cp(car, 0.5, 0.05, 0.28, sx * 0.66, 0.34, -1.98, HEAD_C, Enum.Material.Neon, { shadow = false })
		cp(car, 0.42, 0.016, 0.06, sx * 0.72, 0.36, -2.02, HEAD_C, Enum.Material.Neon, { shadow = false })
		local list = sx < 0 and car.sigL or car.sigR
		list[#list + 1] = cp(car, 0.12, 0.06, 0.05, sx * 0.76, 0.3, -1.7, AMBER_OFF, Enum.Material.Neon, { shadow = false })
		list[#list + 1] = cp(car, 0.1, 0.02, 0.02, sx * 0.62, 0.6, 2.3, AMBER_OFF, Enum.Material.Neon, { shadow = false })
		cp(car, 0.022, 0.13, 0.02, sx * 0.79, 0.66, 2.31, TAIL_C, Enum.Material.Neon, { shadow = false })
		cp(car, 0.16, 0.022, 0.02, sx * 0.71, 0.6, 2.31, TAIL_C, Enum.Material.Neon, { shadow = false })
	end
	cp(car, 1.56, 0.022, 0.02, 0, 0.715, 2.31, TAIL_C, Enum.Material.Neon, { shadow = false })
	for k = 0, 5 do cp(car, 1.5, 0.007, 0.012, 0, 0.45 + k * 0.036, 2.306, ACCENT_DEF) end -- mesh bars
	cp(car, 0.31, 0.155, 0.012, 0, 0.375, 2.32, PLATE_C)
	for _, w in ipairs(wheels) do
		for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do wheel(car, sx * (W / 2 - 0.31 / 2 - 0.02), -w, wr, 0.31, cf.rim, CALIPER, 5) end
	end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------- garage looks: finishes, body kits, decals and lights
local CARB = Color3.fromRGB(18, 19, 21)
local GOLD = Color3.fromRGB(205, 165, 40)
local function carSpec(car)
	if car.spec then return car.spec end
	local L, W = car.L, car.W
	return { W = W, L = L, headS = L / 2 - 0.05, tailS = -L / 2 + 0.05, tailY = 0.72, clear = 0.2, wheels = { L * 0.3, -L * 0.3 }, wr = 0.35, tireW = 0.3 }
end
-- top of a body outline at s, or nil when s is outside it
local function topAt(sp, s)
	if not sp.body then return nil end
	local _, hi = envelope(sp.body, s)
	if hi == -math.huge then return nil end
	return hi
end
-- paint on any car: matte, chrome or the colour-shifting finish (the shift itself is animated every frame in the garage code)
local function applyFinish(car, id)
	car.shift = false
	if id == "pnt_matte" or id == "pnt_chrome" then
		for _, p in ipairs(car.pp or {}) do
			if id == "pnt_matte" then p.Color = Color3.fromRGB(26, 27, 30); p.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic; p.Reflectance = 0
			else p.Color = Color3.fromRGB(206, 211, 219); p.Material = Enum.Material.Metal; p.Reflectance = 0.55 end
		end
	elseif id == "pnt_shift" then
		car.shift = true
		for _, p in ipairs(car.pp or {}) do p.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic; p.Reflectance = 0.22 end
	end
end
local function frontRearWheel(sp) return sp.wheels[1], sp.wheels[#sp.wheels] end
local function kitParts(car, sp, id)
	local W, hs, ts = sp.W, sp.headS, sp.tailS
	local wf, wrr = frontRearWheel(sp)
	local rearTop = (topAt(sp, ts + 0.2) or 0.82)
	if id == "kit_street" then
		cp(car, W * 0.98, 0.05, 0.3, 0, 0.1, -(hs + 0.05), CARB) -- front splitter
		for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
			cp(car, 0.07, 0.13, (wf - wrr) - 0.7, sx * (W / 2 - 0.005), sp.clear + 0.05, -(wf + wrr) / 2, CARB) -- side skirts
		end
		cp(car, W * 0.74, 0.05, 0.2, 0, rearTop + 0.02, -(ts + 0.12), car.paint) -- rear lip
		cp(car, W * 0.8, 0.07, 0.22, 0, 0.17, -(ts - 0.05), CARB) -- rear valance
	elseif id == "kit_track" then
		cp(car, W * 1.02, 0.06, 0.42, 0, 0.1, -(hs + 0.08), CARB) -- big splitter
		cp(car, W * 0.9, 0.04, 0.1, 0, 0.17, -(hs + 0.2), car.paint)
		for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
			cp(car, 0.04, 0.16, 0.36, sx * (W / 2 - 0.12), 0.3, -(hs - 0.1), CARB) -- canards
			cp(car, 0.07, 0.13, (wf - wrr) - 0.5, sx * (W / 2 - 0.005), sp.clear + 0.05, -(wf + wrr) / 2, CARB)
			cp(car, 0.05, 0.44, 0.6, sx * (W * 0.46), rearTop + 0.44, -(ts + 0.34), CARB) -- wing end plates
			cp(car, 0.08, 0.42, 0.12, sx * (W * 0.3), rearTop + 0.22, -(ts + 0.34), CARB) -- uprights
		end
		cp(car, W * 0.98, 0.05, 0.5, 0, rearTop + 0.46, -(ts + 0.34), car.paint, nil, { refl = 0.14 }) -- wing plane
		cp(car, W * 0.98, 0.03, 0.14, 0, rearTop + 0.5, -(ts + 0.2), CARB)
		cp(car, W * 0.74, 0.1, 0.38, 0, 0.2, -(ts - 0.08), CARB) -- diffuser
	end
end
-- colour stripes that follow the body outline over the hood, roof and boot (skipped over the glass)
local function stripes(car, sp, col)
	if not sp.body then return end
	local top, bot = -math.huge, math.huge
	for _, p in ipairs(sp.body) do top = math.max(top, p[1]); bot = math.min(bot, p[1]) end
	local glass, paint = {}, { sp.body }
	for _, sh in ipairs(sp.shells or {}) do if sh.mat == "glass" then glass[#glass + 1] = sh.pts else paint[#paint + 1] = sh.pts end end
	local function hiAt(s)
		local best
		for _, poly in ipairs(paint) do local _, hi = envelope(poly, s); if hi ~= -math.huge and (not best or hi > best) then best = hi end end
		if best then
			for _, poly in ipairs(glass) do local _, hi = envelope(poly, s); if hi ~= -math.huge and hi > best + 0.02 then return nil end end
		end
		return best
	end
	local step = 0.22
	local s = top - 0.1
	while s - step > bot + 0.1 do
		local a, b = hiAt(s - 0.001), hiAt(s - step + 0.001)
		if a and b then
			for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do topped(car, 0.15, sx * 0.17, math.min(a, b) - 0.03, a + 0.012, b + 0.012, s, s - step, col, nil, { shadow = false }) end
		end
		s -= step
	end
end
local function sideY(sp) local lo, hi = envelope(sp.body or { { 1, 0.3 }, { 1, 0.8 }, { -1, 0.8 }, { -1, 0.3 } }, 0); return lo + (hi - lo) * 0.5, lo, hi end
local function decals(car, sp, id, col)
	local W, L = sp.W, sp.L
	local y, lo = sideY(sp)
	if id == "dec_stripes" then stripes(car, sp, col)
	elseif id == "dec_flash" then
		for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
			cp(car, 0.012, 0.11, L * 0.46, sx * (W / 2 + 0.008), y, 0, col, nil, { shadow = false })
			cp(car, 0.012, 0.04, L * 0.34, sx * (W / 2 + 0.008), y - 0.12, -0.1, col, nil, { shadow = false })
		end
	elseif id == "dec_roundel" then
		for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
			local d = cp(car, 0.012, 0.5, 0.5, sx * (W / 2 + 0.01), y, -0.25, Color3.fromRGB(244, 244, 241), nil, { shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder, shadow = false })
			cp(car, 0.014, 0.42, 0.42, sx * (W / 2 + 0.012), y, -0.25, col, nil, { shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder, shadow = false })
			local g = Instance.new("SurfaceGui"); g.Face = sx > 0 and Enum.NormalId.Right or Enum.NormalId.Left; g.SizingMode = Enum.SurfaceGuiSizingMode.PixelsPerStud; g.PixelsPerStud = 50
			local t = Instance.new("TextLabel"); t.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1); t.BackgroundTransparency = 1; t.Text = "07"; t.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack; t.TextScaled = true
			t.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(244, 244, 241); t.Parent = g
			local gp = cp(car, 0.016, 0.3, 0.3, sx * (W / 2 + 0.014), y, -0.25, col, nil, { shadow = false, tr = 1 })
			g.Parent = gp
		end
	elseif id == "dec_hazard" then
		local wf, wrr = frontRearWheel(sp)
		local n = 9
		local len = ((wf - wrr) - 0.5) / n
		for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
			for k = 0, n - 1 do
				cp(car, 0.012, 0.085, len, sx * (W / 2 + 0.006), lo + 0.16, -(wrr + 0.25 + (k + 0.5) * len), k % 2 == 0 and Color3.fromRGB(245, 181, 36) or CARB, nil, { shadow = false })
			end
		end
	end
end
local function lights(car, sp, id, col)
	if id ~= "lit_glow" and id ~= "lit_neon" then return end
	local W, L = sp.W, sp.L
	cp(car, W * 1.9, 0.03, L * 1.25, 0, 0.02, 0, col, Enum.Material.Neon, { shape = Enum.PartType.Ball, tr = 0.8, shadow = false })
	local bar
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do bar = cp(car, 0.05, 0.03, L * 0.72, sx * (W / 2 - 0.12), 0.1, 0, col, Enum.Material.Neon, { shadow = false }) end
	cp(car, W * 0.8, 0.03, 0.05, 0, 0.1, -(sp.headS - 0.3), col, Enum.Material.Neon, { shadow = false })
	cp(car, W * 0.8, 0.03, 0.05, 0, 0.1, -(sp.tailS + 0.3), col, Enum.Material.Neon, { shadow = false })
	local pl = Instance.new("PointLight"); pl.Color = col; pl.Range = id == "lit_neon" and 16 or 12; pl.Brightness = id == "lit_neon" and 2.6 or 1.8; pl.Shadows = false; pl.Parent = bar
	if id == "lit_neon" then
		for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
			local tp = cp(car, 0.5, 0.05, 0.05, sx * (W / 2 - 0.34), sp.tailY + 0.12, -(sp.tailS - 0.08), col, Enum.Material.Neon, { shadow = false })
			local a0, a1 = Instance.new("Attachment"), Instance.new("Attachment")
			a0.Position = Vector3.new(0, 0.06 * S, 0); a1.Position = Vector3.new(0, -0.06 * S, 0); a0.Parent = tp; a1.Parent = tp
			local tr = Instance.new("Trail"); tr.Attachment0 = a0; tr.Attachment1 = a1; tr.Lifetime = 0.7; tr.LightEmission = 1; tr.FaceCamera = true
			tr.Color = ColorSequence.new(col); tr.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.2), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) })
			tr.WidthScale = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.1) }); tr.Parent = tp
		end
	end
end
-- called by buildCar once the base car exists (the wheel style is read earlier, by wheel())
local function dressCar(car, look)
	local sp = carSpec(car)
	local col = CATALOG.COLOURS[look.col or 1].c
	applyFinish(car, look.paint)
	kitParts(car, sp, look.kit)
	decals(car, sp, look.decal, col)
	lights(car, sp, look.lights, col)
end

BUILD.or1 = function(car, c) buildOr1(car, c, CFG.or1) end

CAR_DIM = {
	sedan = { L = 4.85, W = 1.86 }, hatch = { L = 4.2, W = 1.78 }, suv = { L = 4.78, W = 1.93 }, pickup = { L = 5.6, W = 2.0 },
	van = { L = 5.9, W = 2.0 }, semi = { L = 19.1, W = 2.6 }, police = { L = 4.85, W = 1.86 },
	vanta = { L = 4.62, W = 1.93 }, strada = { L = 4.95, W = 1.98 }, kestrel = { L = 4.55, W = 2.04 }, phantom = { L = 4.78, W = 1.98 },
	apex = { L = 4.85, W = 2.08 }, or1 = { L = 4.66, W = 2.04 },
}
local PLAYER_KINDS = { vanta = true, strada = true, or1 = true, kestrel = true, phantom = true, apex = true }

-- 15 more drivable cars built on the existing bodies with their own colours: id, body, dimensions from, rim, accent, led, glow
local NEWCARS = {
	{ "pip", "hatch", "hatch", cfg("b5bcc2", "1b1d20", "ff2a1a") },
	{ "pipr", "hatch", "hatch", cfg("e24a1a", "e24a1a", "ff2a1a") },
	{ "meridian", "sedan", "sedan", cfg("a9afb5", "1b1d20", "ff2a1a") },
	{ "vanline", "van", "van", cfg("9aa1a7", "1b1d20", "ff2a1a") },
	{ "ridgeback", "suv", "suv", cfg("2a2d31", "d9a400", "ff2a1a") },
	{ "hauler", "pickup", "pickup", cfg("2a2d31", "e24a1a", "ff2a1a") },
	{ "vantars", "sport", "vanta", cfg("d4af37", "d4af37", "ff2a1a") },
	{ "gt500", "muscle", "strada", cfg("c9ced3", "1b4fd8", "ff2a1a", "1b4fd8") },
	{ "kestrels", "wedge", "kestrel", cfg("e8e8e4", "e24a1a", "ffffff", "ff7a2a") },
	{ "phantomx", "ev", "phantom", cfg("141516", "ff4fa8", "ff4fa8", "ff4fa8") },
	{ "apexr", "hyper", "apex", cfg("1b1d20", "22d3ff", "22d3ff", "22d3ff") },
	{ "or2", "or1", "or1", cfg("1a1b1d", "ff7a1a", "ff2a1a") },
	{ "nova", "hyper", "apex", cfg("e8e8e4", "7ff0ff", "7ff0ff", "22d3ff") },
	{ "specter", "ev", "phantom", cfg("6a3fd8", "8a4dff", "8a4dff", "8a4dff") },
	{ "eclipse", "or1", "or1", cfg("c9a227", "c9a227", "ff2a1a", "c9a227") },
}
for _, n in ipairs(NEWCARS) do
	local sp = WSPEC[n[1]]
	CAR_DIM[n[1]] = { L = sp.L, W = sp.W }; PLAYER_KINDS[n[1]] = true
	BUILD[n[1]] = function(car, c) buildWeb(car, c, n[1], n[4]) end
end
function buildCar(kind, color, look)
	local d = CAR_DIM[kind]
	local car = { kind = kind, L = d.L, W = d.W, paint = color, hi = PLAYER_KINDS[kind] or kind == "police" }
	car.look = look
	car.model = Instance.new("Model"); car.model.Name = "Car_" .. kind
	local root = Instance.new("Part"); root.Anchored = true; root.CanCollide = false; root.CanQuery = false; root.CanTouch = false
	root.Transparency = 1; root.Size = Vector3.new(0.4, 0.3, 0.4) * S; root.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 300, 0); root.Parent = car.model
	car.root = root; car.model.PrimaryPart = root
	BUILD[kind](car, color)
	if look then dressCar(car, look) end
	-- the real outer box of the finished model (half width, half length, height), used to keep every corner of it above the road
	do
		local bcf, bsize = car.model:GetBoundingBox()
		local rel = root.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(bcf).Position
		car.bw = (math.abs(rel.X) + bsize.X / 2) / S; car.bl = (math.abs(rel.Z) + bsize.Z / 2) / S; car.bt = (rel.Y + bsize.Y / 2) / S
	end
	car.model.Parent = world
	if PLAYER_KINDS[kind] then
		local beam = Instance.new("SpotLight"); beam.Face = Enum.NormalId.Front; beam.Angle = 55; beam.Range = 60; beam.Brightness = 3
		beam.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 244, 220); beam.Shadows = true; beam.Parent = car.root
	end
	return car
end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------- lighting
local rainOn, todNow, streetLights, applyWeather
do
for _, e in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do if e:IsA("PostEffect") or e:IsA("Atmosphere") or e:IsA("Sky") then e:Destroy() end end
local atmosphere = Instance.new("Atmosphere"); atmosphere.Parent = Lighting
local sky = Instance.new("Sky"); sky.SunAngularSize = 14; sky.MoonAngularSize = 9; sky.StarCount = 1500; sky.Parent = Lighting
local cc = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect"); cc.Parent = Lighting
local bloom = Instance.new("BloomEffect"); bloom.Intensity = 0.55; bloom.Size = 30; bloom.Threshold = 1.15; bloom.Parent = Lighting
local sun = Instance.new("SunRaysEffect"); sun.Intensity = 0.1; sun.Spread = 0.6; sun.Parent = Lighting
local dof = Instance.new("DepthOfFieldEffect"); dof.FarIntensity = 0.22; dof.FocusDistance = 90; dof.InFocusRadius = 160; dof.NearIntensity = 0; dof.Parent = Lighting
local clouds = Workspace.Terrain:FindFirstChildOfClass("Clouds") or Instance.new("Clouds")
clouds.Parent = Workspace.Terrain
local LIT = { atmosphere = atmosphere, sky = sky, cc = cc, bloom = bloom, sun = sun, clouds = clouds }
rainOn = false
todNow = "dusk"
streetLights = {}
function applyWeather()
	local night = todNow == "night"
	Lighting.ClockTime = todNow == "day" and 13.2 or night and 20.9 or (rainOn and 17.0 or 17.72)
	Lighting.GeographicLatitude = 34
	Lighting.Brightness = night and 0.5 or (rainOn and 1.6 or 3.2)
	Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 1; Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 1
	Lighting.ShadowSoftness = 0.15
	Lighting.Ambient = rainOn and Color3.fromRGB(62, 66, 76) or Color3.fromRGB(70, 60, 66)
	Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = rainOn and Color3.fromRGB(96, 102, 114) or Color3.fromRGB(128, 110, 108)
	Lighting.ExposureCompensation = night and 0.35 or (rainOn and -0.15 or 0.1)
	for _, l in ipairs(streetLights) do l.Brightness = night and 3.5 or 2.2; l.Range = night and 56 or 48 end
	atmosphere.Density = rainOn and 0.42 or 0.2; atmosphere.Offset = rainOn and 0.1 or 0.22
	atmosphere.Color = rainOn and Color3.fromRGB(160, 166, 178) or Color3.fromRGB(236, 186, 150)
	atmosphere.Decay = rainOn and Color3.fromRGB(96, 100, 112) or Color3.fromRGB(122, 86, 92)
	atmosphere.Glare = rainOn and 0 or 0.45; atmosphere.Haze = rainOn and 2.8 or 1.3
	clouds.Cover = rainOn and 0.92 or 0.55; clouds.Density = rainOn and 0.85 or 0.45
	clouds.Color = rainOn and Color3.fromRGB(130, 134, 142) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 236, 222)
	sun.Intensity = rainOn and 0 or 0.1
	cc.Saturation = rainOn and -0.2 or 0.12; cc.Contrast = 0.12; cc.Brightness = rainOn and -0.02 or 0.01
	cc.TintColor = rainOn and Color3.fromRGB(214, 224, 240) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 242, 230)
	if TERRAIN.light then TERRAIN.light(LIT, night, rainOn, todNow) end -- the current map's own sky and light
end
applyWeather()

end

---------------------------------------------------------------- road, scenery and maps
local updateRoad, setWet, setDivided
local medianParts, yellowParts
do
local segs = {}
local roadParts = {}
local rng = Random.new(7)
local V, MAT = Vector3.new, Enum.Material
local function at(x, y, z) return CFrame.new(x * S, y * S, z * S) end
local function col(r, g, b) return Color3.fromRGB(r, g, b) end
local function off(a, b) return (rng:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and -1 or 1) * rng:NextNumber(OUTER_WALL + a, OUTER_WALL + b) end
local function rz(L) return rng:NextNumber(-L / 2 + 2, L / 2 - 2) end
local function chance(p) return rng:NextNumber() < p end
local function cyl(m, d, h, x, y, z, c, mat, o) -- upright cylinder, d across and h tall
	o = o or {}; o.shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder
	return mk(m, V(h, d, d), at(x, y, z) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi / 2), c, mat, o)
end
local function ball(m, d, x, y, z, c, mat, o)
	o = o or {}; o.shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
	return mk(m, V(d, d, d), at(x, y, z), c, mat, o)
end
local function blob(m, sx, sy, sz, x, y, z, c, o) -- squashed sphere
	local p = mk(m, V(sx, sy, sz), at(x, y, z), c, nil, o)
	local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh"); mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.Sphere; mesh.Parent = p
	return p
end

---------------------------------------------------------------- scenery pieces
local GREENS = { col(46, 84, 44), col(58, 96, 48), col(38, 70, 40), col(72, 102, 52) }
local NEONS = { col(34, 211, 255), col(255, 60, 200), col(150, 90, 255), col(255, 170, 40) }
local function tree(m, x, z, h)
	if rng:NextNumber() < 0.45 then -- pine: trunk with stacked, shrinking canopy layers
		mk(m, V(0.4, h * 0.5, 0.4), at(x, h * 0.25, z), col(66, 46, 32), MAT.Wood)
		local g = col(30, 62, 40)
		for k = 0, 3 do
			local r = h * (0.42 - k * 0.09)
			mk(m, V(r, r * 0.8, r), at(x, h * 0.35 + k * h * 0.17, z), g, MAT.Grass, { shape = Enum.PartType.Ball })
		end
	else -- broadleaf: trunk with a cluster of canopy balls
		mk(m, V(0.45, h * 0.6, 0.45), at(x, h * 0.3, z), col(78, 58, 40), MAT.Wood)
		local g = GREENS[rng:NextInteger(1, #GREENS)]
		for k = 1, 3 do
			local r = h * rng:NextNumber(0.38, 0.5)
			local ox, oz = rng:NextNumber(-0.25, 0.25) * h, rng:NextNumber(-0.25, 0.25) * h
			mk(m, V(r, r, r), at(x + ox, h * rng:NextNumber(0.68, 0.85), z + oz), g:Lerp(Color3.new(0, 0, 0), rng:NextNumber(0, 0.15)), MAT.Grass, { shape = Enum.PartType.Ball })
		end
	end
end
local function snowPine(m, x, z, h)
	mk(m, V(0.4, h * 0.5, 0.4), at(x, h * 0.25, z), col(60, 44, 34), MAT.Wood)
	for k = 0, 2 do
		local r = h * (0.46 - k * 0.11)
		local y = h * 0.36 + k * h * 0.2
		ball(m, r, x, y, z, col(34, 66, 52), MAT.Grass)
		if k > 0 then ball(m, r * 0.86, x, y + r * 0.2, z, col(240, 244, 250), MAT.Snow, { shadow = false }) end
	end
end
local function blossom(m, x, z, h)
	mk(m, V(0.35, h * 0.55, 0.35), at(x, h * 0.27, z), col(58, 42, 40), MAT.Wood)
	for _ = 1, 3 do
		local r = h * rng:NextNumber(0.34, 0.46)
		ball(m, r, x + rng:NextNumber(-0.22, 0.22) * h, h * rng:NextNumber(0.62, 0.8), z + rng:NextNumber(-0.22, 0.22) * h, col(246, 170, 196), MAT.Grass)
	end
end
local function torii(m, x, z, h, span) -- span: half-width between the posts (0 = a small roadside gate facing the road)
	local RED, DARK = col(204, 44, 32), col(34, 30, 34)
	local w = span > 0 and span or h * 0.42
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do cyl(m, h * 0.085, h, x + sx * w, h / 2, z, RED) end
	mk(m, V(w * 2 + h * 0.5, h * 0.06, h * 0.1), at(x, h * 0.8, z), RED)
	mk(m, V(w * 2 + h * 0.75, h * 0.07, h * 0.13), at(x, h * 1.0, z), RED)
	mk(m, V(w * 2 + h * 0.9, h * 0.05, h * 0.17), at(x, h * 1.06, z), DARK)
end
local function lantern(m, x, z)
	local STONE = col(150, 152, 158)
	mk(m, V(0.7, 0.3, 0.7), at(x, 0.15, z), STONE, MAT.Slate)
	mk(m, V(0.28, 1.1, 0.28), at(x, 0.85, z), STONE, MAT.Slate)
	mk(m, V(0.6, 0.5, 0.6), at(x, 1.65, z), col(255, 196, 120), MAT.Neon, { shadow = false })
	mk(m, V(1.0, 0.22, 1.0), at(x, 2.0, z), STONE, MAT.Slate)
	mk(m, V(1.1, 0.12, 1.1), at(x, 2.16, z), col(240, 244, 250), MAT.Snow, { shadow = false })
end
local function pagoda(m, x, z)
	local y, w = 0, rng:NextNumber(9, 12)
	for _ = 1, 4 do
		mk(m, V(w, 3.2, w), at(x, y + 1.6, z), col(232, 226, 214))
		mk(m, V(w + 3.4, 0.5, w + 3.4), at(x, y + 3.45, z), col(52, 46, 52))
		mk(m, V(w + 3.0, 0.22, w + 3.0), at(x, y + 3.8, z), col(240, 244, 250), MAT.Snow, { shadow = false })
		y += 3.9; w *= 0.8
	end
	cyl(m, 0.4, 5, x, y + 2.5, z, col(212, 175, 55), MAT.Metal)
end
local function cactus(m, x, z, h)
	local G = col(70, 118, 66)
	cyl(m, h * 0.17, h, x, h / 2, z, G, MAT.Grass)
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
		if chance(0.75) then
			local ay, al = h * rng:NextNumber(0.35, 0.55), h * rng:NextNumber(0.25, 0.4)
			mk(m, V(h * 0.24, h * 0.11, h * 0.11), at(x + sx * h * 0.16, ay, z), G, MAT.Grass)
			cyl(m, h * 0.11, al, x + sx * h * 0.28, ay + al / 2, z, G, MAT.Grass)
		end
	end
end
local function butte(m, x, z, w, h)
	local d = w * rng:NextNumber(0.7, 1.1)
	mk(m, V(w, h * 0.55, d), at(x, h * 0.275 - 0.3, z), col(176, 96, 62), MAT.Sandstone)
	mk(m, V(w * 0.78, h * 0.3, d * 0.78), at(x, h * 0.7 - 0.3, z), col(190, 108, 68), MAT.Sandstone)
	mk(m, V(w * 0.66, h * 0.15, d * 0.66), at(x, h * 0.925 - 0.3, z), col(150, 80, 56), MAT.Sandstone)
end
local function palm(m, x, z, h)
	local lean = rng:NextNumber(-0.14, 0.14)
	local base = at(x, 0, z) * CFrame.Angles(lean, rng:NextNumber(0, 6.28), lean * 0.6)
	mk(m, V(h, 0.45, 0.45), base * CFrame.new(0, h / 2 * S, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.pi / 2), col(122, 96, 70), MAT.Wood, { shape = Enum.PartType.Cylinder })
	local top = base * CFrame.new(0, h * S, 0)
	for k = 0, 5 do
		mk(m, V(0.9, 0.1, h * 0.42), top * CFrame.Angles(0, k * math.pi / 3, 0) * CFrame.Angles(-0.45, 0, 0) * CFrame.new(0, 0, -h * 0.2 * S), col(52, 120, 58), MAT.Grass, { shadow = false })
	end
end
local function tower(m, z)
	local w, d, h = rng:NextNumber(14, 30), rng:NextNumber(14, 30), rng:NextNumber(40, 170)
	local x = (rng:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and -1 or 1) * (OUTER_WALL + w / 2 + rng:NextNumber(6, 110))
	local c = NEONS[rng:NextInteger(1, #NEONS)]
	mk(m, V(w, h, d), at(x, h / 2 - 0.3, z), col(18, 20, 30):Lerp(col(40, 44, 64), rng:NextNumber()), nil, { refl = 0.12 })
	mk(m, V(w + 0.5, 1.2, d + 0.5), at(x, h * rng:NextNumber(0.3, 0.6), z), c, MAT.Neon, { shadow = false })
	mk(m, V(w + 0.5, 0.8, d + 0.5), at(x, h - 0.6, z), c, MAT.Neon, { shadow = false })
	mk(m, V(0.5, h * 0.8, 1.2), at(x - (x > 0 and 1 or -1) * (w / 2 + 0.2), h * 0.5, z), NEONS[rng:NextInteger(1, #NEONS)], MAT.Neon, { shadow = false })
	if chance(0.5) then mk(m, V(0.4, h * 0.25, 0.4), at(x, h * 1.125, z), col(255, 60, 60), MAT.Neon, { shadow = false }) end
end

---------------------------------------------------------------- the maps: palette, roadside scenery, horizon and light for each one
local THEMES = {}
THEMES.interstate = {
	road = col(48, 49, 52), shoulder = col(62, 62, 64), ground = col(74, 88, 54), groundMat = MAT.Grass, verge = col(104, 98, 86), vergeMat = MAT.Pebble,
	line = col(240, 240, 232), yline = col(240, 190, 40), rail = col(176, 182, 188), post = col(120, 126, 132), barrier = col(176, 172, 164),
	lamp = col(255, 220, 160), lampLight = col(255, 205, 150),
	hill = { a = col(70, 92, 62), b = col(110, 120, 130), mat = MAT.Grass, mesh = Enum.MeshType.Sphere, s = V(1, 1, 1) },
	scenery = function(m, L)
		for _ = 1, 9 do tree(m, off(6, 70), rng:NextNumber(-L / 2, L / 2), rng:NextNumber(6, 12)) end
		for _ = 1, 5 do
			local r = rng:NextNumber(1, 2.2)
			mk(m, V(r, r * 0.7, r), at(off(3, 9), r * 0.2, rng:NextNumber(-L / 2, L / 2)), GREENS[rng:NextInteger(1, #GREENS)], MAT.Grass, { shape = Enum.PartType.Ball })
		end
		if chance(0.5) then
			local r = rng:NextNumber(1.5, 3.5)
			mk(m, V(r * 1.4, r, r), at(off(8, 40), r * 0.3, rng:NextNumber(-L / 2, L / 2)) * CFrame.Angles(0, rng:NextNumber(0, 6), 0.2), col(120, 112, 104), MAT.Rock)
		end
	end,
}
THEMES.mesa = {
	road = col(72, 64, 60), shoulder = col(96, 80, 68), ground = col(198, 150, 98), groundMat = MAT.Sand, verge = col(170, 122, 82), vergeMat = MAT.Sand,
	line = col(236, 228, 208), yline = col(240, 190, 40), rail = col(170, 160, 150), post = col(120, 106, 96), barrier = col(190, 170, 150),
	lamp = col(255, 214, 150), lampLight = col(255, 196, 130),
	hill = { a = col(176, 96, 62), b = col(206, 138, 96), mat = MAT.Sandstone, mesh = Enum.MeshType.Brick, s = V(0.7, 0.9, 0.7) },
	light = function(L)
		L.atmosphere.Color = col(244, 196, 150); L.atmosphere.Decay = col(196, 112, 80); L.clouds.Cover = 0.22
		L.cc.TintColor = col(255, 238, 214)
	end,
	scenery = function(m, L)
		for _, z in ipairs({ -20, 20 }) do -- telephone poles
			mk(m, V(0.28, 9, 0.28), at(OUTER_WALL + 4.5, 4.5, z), col(92, 68, 48), MAT.Wood)
			mk(m, V(2.6, 0.18, 0.18), at(OUTER_WALL + 4.5, 8.3, z), col(92, 68, 48), MAT.Wood)
		end
		for _ = 1, 5 do cactus(m, off(5, 60), rz(L), rng:NextNumber(2.5, 5.5)) end
		for _ = 1, 2 do butte(m, off(50, 160), rz(L), rng:NextNumber(18, 42), rng:NextNumber(14, 42)) end
		for _ = 1, 5 do
			local r = rng:NextNumber(0.8, 3)
			mk(m, V(r * 1.3, r, r), at(off(4, 50), r * 0.25, rz(L)) * CFrame.Angles(0, rng:NextNumber(0, 6), 0.2), col(168, 104, 74), MAT.Rock)
		end
		for _ = 1, 4 do ball(m, rng:NextNumber(0.6, 1.3), off(3, 30), 0.2, rz(L), col(176, 160, 104), MAT.Grass, { shadow = false }) end
	end,
}
THEMES.coast = {
	road = col(56, 56, 60), shoulder = col(80, 78, 76), ground = col(226, 204, 160), groundMat = MAT.Sand, verge = col(206, 188, 150), vergeMat = MAT.Sand,
	line = col(244, 244, 236), yline = col(240, 190, 40), rail = col(236, 236, 230), post = col(170, 170, 168), barrier = col(214, 208, 196),
	lamp = col(255, 214, 170), lampLight = col(255, 190, 140), sea = true,
	hill = { a = col(96, 124, 80), b = col(150, 140, 110), mat = MAT.Grass, mesh = Enum.MeshType.Sphere, s = V(1, 0.8, 1), oneSide = true },

		L.atmosphere.Color = col(255, 198, 162); L.atmosphere.Decay = col(232, 124, 112); L.clouds.Cover = 0.4
		L.cc.TintColor = col(255, 236, 222); L.cc.Saturation = 0.22
	end,
	scenery = function(m, L)
