		for _ = 1, 3 do -- beach umbrellas
			local x, z, c = rng:NextNumber(OUTER_WALL + 8, OUTER_WALL + 40), rz(L), NEONS[rng:NextInteger(1, #NEONS)]
			mk(m, V(0.1, 2.2, 0.1), at(x, 1.1, z), col(240, 240, 240))
			blob(m, 3, 0.7, 3, x, 2.3, z, c:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.25))
		end
		for _ = 1, 4 do
			local r = rng:NextNumber(1, 2.4)
			mk(m, V(r, r * 0.7, r), at(rng:NextNumber(OUTER_WALL + 3, OUTER_WALL + 30), r * 0.2, rz(L)), GREENS[rng:NextInteger(1, #GREENS)], MAT.Grass, { shape = Enum.PartType.Ball })
		end
		for _ = 1, rng:NextInteger(1, 2) do -- sea stacks standing in the water, some with a lighthouse
			local x, z, d, top = -rng:NextNumber(OUTER_WALL + 35, OUTER_WALL + 150), rz(L), rng:NextNumber(8, 18), rng:NextNumber(-1, 7)
			cyl(m, d, 70, x, top - 35, z, col(120, 104, 92), MAT.Slate)
			if chance(0.2) then
				cyl(m, 3.2, 12, x, top + 6, z, col(240, 240, 236)); cyl(m, 3.3, 2.4, x, top + 7, z, col(204, 44, 32))
				ball(m, 2.4, x, top + 13, z, col(255, 236, 170), MAT.Neon, { shadow = false })
			end
		end
	end,
}
THEMES.yuki = {
	road = col(58, 60, 66), shoulder = col(196, 202, 212), ground = col(236, 240, 246), groundMat = MAT.Snow, verge = col(224, 230, 238), vergeMat = MAT.Snow,
	line = col(246, 246, 240), yline = col(244, 200, 60), rail = col(186, 192, 200), post = col(130, 136, 144), barrier = col(206, 210, 216),
	lamp = col(255, 206, 150), lampLight = col(255, 190, 130), snow = true,
	hill = { a = col(238, 242, 250), b = col(186, 202, 226), mat = MAT.Snow, mesh = Enum.MeshType.Sphere, s = V(0.8, 1.7, 0.8) },
	light = function(L, night)
		L.atmosphere.Color = col(226, 232, 244); L.atmosphere.Decay = col(150, 166, 192); L.atmosphere.Density = 0.34; L.atmosphere.Haze = 2.2
		L.clouds.Cover = 0.86; L.clouds.Color = col(236, 238, 246)
		L.cc.TintColor = col(232, 240, 255); L.cc.Saturation = -0.05
		Lighting.Ambient = col(92, 98, 112); Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = night and col(110, 118, 136) or col(152, 160, 174)
	end,
	scenery = function(m, L)
		for _ = 1, 7 do snowPine(m, off(6, 70), rz(L), rng:NextNumber(6, 12)) end
		for _ = 1, 2 do blossom(m, off(5, 40), rz(L), rng:NextNumber(4, 6.5)) end
		for _ = 1, 2 do lantern(m, off(2.2, 3.2), rz(L)) end
		if chance(0.25) then torii(m, 0, rng:NextNumber(-25, 5), 14, OUTER_WALL + 2.6) -- a great gate over the whole road
		elseif chance(0.4) then torii(m, off(8, 30), rz(L), rng:NextNumber(4.5, 7), 0) end
		if chance(0.3) then pagoda(m, off(50, 120), rz(L)) end
		for _ = 1, 3 do local r = rng:NextNumber(1.2, 3); blob(m, r * 1.5, r * 0.7, r * 1.2, off(4, 40), 0.05, rz(L), col(244, 246, 252)) end
	end,
}
THEMES.neon = {
	road = col(26, 27, 34), shoulder = col(36, 38, 48), ground = col(22, 24, 32), groundMat = MAT.Concrete, verge = col(40, 42, 54), vergeMat = MAT.Concrete,
	line = col(170, 240, 255), lineMat = MAT.Neon, yline = col(255, 90, 210), rail = col(70, 76, 96), post = col(50, 54, 70), barrier = col(52, 56, 74),
	lamp = col(120, 230, 255), lampLight = col(110, 210, 255), edge = { col(255, 60, 200), col(34, 211, 255) },
	hill = { a = col(20, 22, 36), b = col(46, 40, 78), mat = MAT.SmoothPlastic, mesh = Enum.MeshType.Brick, s = V(0.22, 3, 0.3) },
	light = function(L, night)
		L.atmosphere.Color = col(140, 104, 220); L.atmosphere.Decay = col(52, 30, 112); L.atmosphere.Density = 0.32; L.atmosphere.Haze = 2
		L.clouds.Cover = 0.8; L.clouds.Color = col(96, 76, 140)
		L.cc.TintColor = col(236, 226, 255); L.cc.Saturation = 0.2; L.bloom.Intensity = 0.95
		Lighting.Ambient = col(72, 54, 104); Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = col(96, 86, 146)
		if not night then Lighting.Brightness = math.min(Lighting.Brightness, 1.6) end
	end,
	scenery = function(m, L)
		for _ = 1, 7 do tower(m, rz(L)) end
		do -- holo billboard
			local sx, z = (rng:NextInteger(0, 1) == 0 and -1 or 1), rz(L)
			local x = sx * (OUTER_WALL + 6)
			for _, dz in ipairs({ -5, 5 }) do mk(m, V(0.35, 8, 0.35), at(x, 4, z + dz), col(50, 54, 70), MAT.Metal) end
			mk(m, V(0.3, 5, 13), at(x, 10, z), NEONS[rng:NextInteger(1, #NEONS)], MAT.Neon, { tr = 0.2, shadow = false })
		end
		if chance(0.35) then -- light arch over the road
			local z, c = rng:NextNumber(-30, 0), NEONS[rng:NextInteger(1, #NEONS)]
			for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do mk(m, V(0.7, 12, 0.7), at(sx * (OUTER_WALL + 1.6), 6, z), col(50, 54, 70), MAT.Metal) end
			mk(m, V((OUTER_WALL + 1.6) * 2, 0.5, 0.5), at(0, 12, z), c, MAT.Neon, { shadow = false })
		end
	end,
}
THEMES.lunar = {
	road = col(66, 70, 80), roadMat = MAT.Concrete, shoulder = col(88, 92, 102), ground = col(124, 126, 132), groundMat = MAT.Slate, verge = col(108, 110, 118), vergeMat = MAT.Slate,
	line = col(210, 240, 255), lineMat = MAT.Neon, yline = col(255, 200, 80), rail = col(196, 202, 212), post = col(150, 156, 166), barrier = col(170, 176, 186),
	lamp = col(210, 235, 255), lampLight = col(200, 225, 255), edge = { col(120, 190, 255), col(120, 190, 255) }, earth = true,
	hill = { a = col(124, 126, 134), b = col(92, 94, 104), mat = MAT.Slate, mesh = Enum.MeshType.Sphere, s = V(1.1, 0.6, 1.1) },
	light = function(L, night, rain, tod)
		-- a black sky full of stars at any hour: the clock stays at midnight, and the "time of day" only sets how hard the light is.
		-- Midnight light is weak, so the ambient light and exposure are pushed up to keep the ground and cars clearly lit.
		local k = tod == "day" and 1 or (night and 0.45 or 0.72)
		Lighting.ClockTime = 0; Lighting.Brightness = 1 + 3 * k
		L.atmosphere.Density = 0; L.atmosphere.Haze = 0; L.atmosphere.Glare = 0
		L.clouds.Enabled = false; L.sky.StarCount = 3000; L.sun.Intensity = 0
		L.cc.TintColor = col(236, 240, 255); L.cc.Saturation = -0.12
		Lighting.Ambient = col(60, 62, 72):Lerp(col(124, 126, 136), k); Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = col(90, 92, 106):Lerp(col(196, 198, 210), k)
		Lighting.ExposureCompensation = 0.4 + 0.7 * k
	end,
	scenery = function(m, L)
		for _ = 1, 3 do -- craters: a pale rim with a dark floor
			local x, z, d = off(18, 90), rz(L), rng:NextNumber(6, 22)
			cyl(m, d * 1.25, 0.1, x, -0.22, z, col(146, 148, 156), MAT.Slate, { shadow = false })
			cyl(m, d, 0.1, x, -0.19, z, col(84, 86, 94), MAT.Slate, { shadow = false })
		end
		for _ = 1, 6 do
			local r = rng:NextNumber(0.6, 3)
			mk(m, V(r * 1.3, r, r), at(off(4, 70), r * 0.2, rz(L)) * CFrame.Angles(0, rng:NextNumber(0, 6), 0.25), col(112, 114, 122), MAT.Rock)
		end
		if chance(0.5) then -- habitat dome with a glowing base ring and an airlock
			local x, z, d = off(22, 80), rz(L), rng:NextNumber(14, 24)
			ball(m, d, x, -0.25, z, col(226, 232, 240), nil, { refl = 0.2 })
			cyl(m, d + 1.2, 0.5, x, 0, z, col(120, 190, 255), MAT.Neon, { shadow = false })
			mk(m, V(4, 3, 4), at(x - (x > 0 and 1 or -1) * d / 2, 1.3, z), col(200, 206, 216), MAT.Metal)
		end
		if chance(0.6) then -- solar panels
			local x, z = off(8, 40), rz(L)
			for k = -1, 1 do
				mk(m, V(0.2, 1.6, 0.2), at(x, 0.8, z + k * 3.4), col(150, 156, 166), MAT.Metal)
				mk(m, V(4.2, 0.12, 2.8), at(x, 1.8, z + k * 3.4) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, x > 0 and 0.5 or -0.5), col(30, 62, 156), nil, { refl = 0.3 })
			end
		end
		if chance(0.4) then -- comms mast
			local x, z = off(10, 60), rz(L)
			mk(m, V(0.5, 22, 0.5), at(x, 11, z), col(170, 176, 186), MAT.Metal)
			local dish = blob(m, 5, 1.2, 5, x, 19, z + 0.6, col(226, 232, 240)); dish.CFrame = dish.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0.9, 0, 0)
			ball(m, 0.7, x, 22.4, z, col(255, 60, 60), MAT.Neon, { shadow = false })
		end
		if chance(0.12) then -- a rocket on its pad
			local x, z = off(60, 130), rz(L)
			cyl(m, 14, 0.4, x, 0, z, col(90, 92, 100), MAT.Concrete)
			cyl(m, 4, 24, x, 13, z, col(240, 240, 244)); ball(m, 4, x, 25, z, col(204, 44, 32))
			for k = 0, 2 do mk(m, V(0.3, 5, 3), at(x, 3, z) * CFrame.Angles(0, k * 2.094, 0) * CFrame.new(0, 0, 2.6 * S), col(204, 44, 32)) end
		end
	end,
}
for id, t in pairs(THEMES) do t.id = id end
local MAP = THEMES.interstate
TERRAIN.map = MAP

medianParts, yellowParts = {}, {}
local function buildSegment()
	local T = MAP
	local m = Instance.new("Model"); m.Name = "Segment"
	local L = SEG_LEN + 0.2 -- a hair of overlap hides the joins; more than that would stick up as a lip on hill crests
	local road = mk(m, V(ROAD_X1 * 2, 0.2, L), at(0, -0.1, 0), T.road, T.roadMat or MAT.Asphalt)
	-- aprons: on bends the square ends of two segments leave a thin wedge open on the outside of the turn. These longer slabs sit well
	-- below the surface (low enough never to poke up through the next segment on a crest) so the wedge shows road and ground, not sky.
	mk(m, V(ROAD_X1 * 2, 0.1, SEG_LEN + 4), at(0, -0.4, 0), T.road, T.roadMat or MAT.Asphalt, { shadow = false })
	if not T.sea then mk(m, V(420, 0.1, SEG_LEN + 8), at(0, -0.85, 0), T.ground, T.groundMat, { shadow = false }) end
	road.Name = "Road"; road.CanQuery = true; roadParts[#roadParts + 1] = road
	m.PrimaryPart = road
	if T.sea then -- land on the right only; a rock cliff drops to the sea on the left
		local x0 = -(OUTER_WALL + 5)
		mk(m, V(210 - x0, 0.3, L), at((210 + x0) / 2, -0.4, 0), T.ground, T.groundMat)
		mk(m, V(2.5, 70, L), at(x0 - 1.2, -35.25, 0), col(120, 104, 92), MAT.Slate)
	else
		mk(m, V(420, 0.3, L), at(0, -0.4, 0), T.ground, T.groundMat)
	end
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
		-- outer shoulder, rumble strip, edge lines (white outside, yellow on the median side)
		local sh = mk(m, V(3.0, 0.2, L), at(sx * 18.1, -0.098, 0), T.shoulder, T.roadMat or MAT.Asphalt)
		sh.CanQuery = true; roadParts[#roadParts + 1] = sh
		mk(m, V(0.45, 0.025, L), at(sx * 17.05, 0.006, 0), T.shoulder:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.1), MAT.DiamondPlate, { shadow = false })
		mk(m, V(0.16, 0.02, L), at(sx * 16.6, 0.008, 0), T.line, T.lineMat, { shadow = false })
		mk(m, V(0.14, 0.02, L), at(sx * 1.8, 0.008, 0), T.yline, T.lineMat, { shadow = false })
		-- lane dashes: 3 m paint every 12 m
		for _, lx in ipairs({ 5.5, 9.2, 12.9 }) do
			for d = 0, SEG_LEN / 12 - 1 do
				mk(m, V(0.14, 0.02, 3), at(sx * lx, 0.007, -SEG_LEN / 2 + 4 + d * 12), T.line, T.lineMat, { shadow = false })
			end
		end
		-- verge, guardrail beam and posts, reflector posts
		mk(m, V(3, 0.25, L), at(sx * (OUTER_WALL + 1.6), -0.22, 0), T.verge, T.vergeMat)
		mk(m, V(0.08, 0.32, L), at(sx * OUTER_WALL, 0.62, 0), T.rail, MAT.DiamondPlate, { refl = 0.1 })
		if T.edge then mk(m, V(0.1, 0.1, L), at(sx * OUTER_WALL, 0.86, 0), T.edge[sx > 0 and 2 or 1], MAT.Neon, { shadow = false }) end
		for z = -SEG_LEN / 2 + 2, SEG_LEN / 2, 6 do
			mk(m, V(0.12, 0.75, 0.12), at(sx * (OUTER_WALL + 0.12), 0.37, z), T.post, MAT.Metal)
		end
		mk(m, V(0.1, 0.9, 0.1), at(sx * (OUTER_WALL + 0.9), 0.45, 0), col(235, 235, 230))
		mk(m, V(0.11, 0.12, 0.11), at(sx * (OUTER_WALL + 0.9), 0.8, 0), sx > 0 and col(255, 150, 40) or col(240, 240, 240), MAT.Neon, { shadow = false })
	end
	-- median: concrete barrier (divided) or double yellow lines (two-way)
	local mb = mk(m, V(0.62, 0.32, L), at(0, 0.16, 0), T.barrier, MAT.Concrete)
	local mt = mk(m, V(0.24, 0.55, L), at(0, 0.58, 0), T.barrier:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.06), MAT.Concrete)
	medianParts[#medianParts + 1] = mb; medianParts[#medianParts + 1] = mt
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
		local y = mk(m, V(0.12, 0.02, L), at(sx * 0.14, 0.008, 0), T.yline, T.lineMat, { shadow = false })
		yellowParts[#yellowParts + 1] = y
	end
	-- street lights on the median, double-armed
	local lz = 20
	mk(m, V(0.25, 10, 0.25), at(0, 5, lz), col(84, 88, 94), MAT.Metal).Name = "Pole"
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
		mk(m, V(3.4, 0.18, 0.18), at(sx * 1.6, 10, lz), col(84, 88, 94), MAT.Metal)
		local head = mk(m, V(0.9, 0.18, 0.45), at(sx * 3.2, 9.9, lz), T.lamp, MAT.Neon, { shadow = false })
		local sl = Instance.new("SpotLight"); sl.Face = Enum.NormalId.Bottom; sl.Angle = 120; sl.Range = 48; sl.Brightness = 2.2; sl.Color = T.lampLight; sl.Parent = head
		streetLights[#streetLights + 1] = sl
	end
	T.scenery(m, L)
	m.Parent = world
	return m
end

-- horizon: huge shapes that ride along with the camera so it never runs out (hills, mesas, snowy peaks or far-off towers, by map)
local hills = {}
for i = 1, 16 do
	local p = Instance.new("Part"); p.Anchored = true; p.CanCollide = false; p.CanQuery = false; p.CanTouch = false; p.CastShadow = false
	p.Size = Vector3.new(4, 4, 4)
	local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh"); mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.Sphere
	local w = rng:NextNumber(180, 340)
	local scale = Vector3.new(w, rng:NextNumber(45, 110), w * rng:NextNumber(0.6, 1)) * S / 4
	mesh.Parent = p
	p.Parent = world
	local side = i % 2 == 0 and -1 or 1
	hills[i] = { part = p, mesh = mesh, scale = scale, x = side * rng:NextNumber(260, 520), z = (i - 8) * 120 + rng:NextNumber(-40, 40) }
end
local function styleHills()
	local H = MAP.hill
	for i, h in ipairs(hills) do
		h.part.Color = H.a:Lerp(H.b, (i % 4) / 6); h.part.Material = H.mat
		h.mesh.MeshType = H.mesh; h.mesh.Scale = h.scale * H.s
		h.part.Transparency = (H.oneSide and h.x < 0) and 1 or 0
	end
end
styleHills()
-- the sea (Sunset Coast) and the Earth (Lunar Loop) stay level and follow the car
local seaParts, earth = {}, nil
for i = 0, 8 do
	local p = Instance.new("Part"); p.Anchored = true; p.CanCollide = false; p.CanQuery = false; p.CanTouch = false; p.CastShadow = false
	p.Size = Vector3.new(2040, 1, 2040); p.Color = col(22, 104, 156); p.Material = MAT.SmoothPlastic; p.Reflectance = 0.28
	p.TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth; p.Transparency = 1; p.Parent = world
	seaParts[i + 1] = { part = p, dx = (i % 3 - 2) * 2040 + 600, dz = (math.floor(i / 3) - 1) * 2040 - 1500 }
end
earth = Instance.new("Part"); earth.Anchored = true; earth.CanCollide = false; earth.CanQuery = false; earth.CanTouch = false; earth.CastShadow = false
earth.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball; earth.Size = Vector3.new(900, 900, 900); earth.Color = col(64, 132, 236); earth.Material = MAT.Neon
earth.Transparency = 1; earth.Parent = world
local function updateHills(f)
	for _, h in ipairs(hills) do
		local z = h.z - (f * 0.15) % 1920 -- slow parallax drift
		if z < -960 then z += 1920 end
		h.part.CFrame = CFrame.new(w2v(h.x, -6, f + z + 700))
	end
	local c = w2v(0, 0, f)
	if MAP.sea then
		local y = -(TERRAIN.amp * 1.9 + 3) * S
		for _, s in ipairs(seaParts) do s.part.CFrame = CFrame.new(c.X + s.dx, y, c.Z + s.dz) end
	end
	if MAP.earth then earth.CFrame = CFrame.new(c + Vector3.new(2100, 1500, -5200)) end
end
local function placeSegment(s, k)
	s.k = k
	s.model:PivotTo(segCF(k) * CFrame.new(0, -0.1 * S, 0)) -- the pivot is the middle of the 0.2 m road slab, so its TOP is exactly the surface cars sit on
end
for i = 1, SEG_COUNT do segs[i] = { model = buildSegment() } end
local terrainKey = ""
function updateRoad(f)
	local key = TERRAIN.amp .. "/" .. TERRAIN.bend
	if key ~= terrainKey then terrainKey = key; for _, s in ipairs(segs) do s.k = nil end end -- terrain changed: re-place everything
	local base = math.floor(f / SEG_LEN) - 3
	for i, s in ipairs(segs) do
		local k = base + ((i - 1 - base) % SEG_COUNT)
		if s.k ~= k then placeSegment(s, k) end
	end
	updateHills(f)
end
function setDivided(on)
	for _, p in ipairs(medianParts) do p.Transparency = on and 0 or 1 end
	for _, p in ipairs(yellowParts) do p.Transparency = on and 1 or 0 end
end
function setWet(on)
	for i, r in ipairs(roadParts) do
		r.Reflectance = on and 0.22 or 0
		local base = r.Name == "Road" and MAP.road or MAP.shoulder
		r.Color = on and base:Lerp(Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 0.3) or base
	end
end
-- switch map: rebuild the 20 road segments in the new style (callers re-apply weather, wet road and the median afterwards)
TERRAIN.setMap = function(id)
	local T = THEMES[id] or THEMES.interstate
	if T == MAP then return false end
	MAP = T; TERRAIN.map = T
	for _, s in ipairs(segs) do s.model:Destroy() end
	table.clear(segs); table.clear(roadParts); table.clear(medianParts); table.clear(yellowParts); table.clear(streetLights)
	rng = Random.new(7)
	for i = 1, SEG_COUNT do segs[i] = { model = buildSegment() } end
	styleHills()
	for _, s in ipairs(seaParts) do s.part.Transparency = T.sea and 0 or 1 end
	earth.Transparency = T.earth and 0 or 1
	return true
end
-- each map's own light, applied on top of the time of day and weather
TERRAIN.light = function(L, night, rain, tod)
	L.clouds.Enabled = true; L.sky.StarCount = 1500; L.bloom.Intensity = 0.55
	if MAP.light then MAP.light(L, night, rain, tod) end
end
-- THE LAST WORD ON "NEVER UNDER THE ROAD": lower the car's whole outer box onto the real road parts (an exact box-against-box test done
-- by the engine) and lift the car if any part of it would be touching or below them.
local castParams = RaycastParams.new(); castParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
local castFor = nil
TERRAIN.settle = function(car)
	if castFor ~= segs[1] then castFor = segs[1]; castParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = roadParts end -- the road was rebuilt
	local cf, RISE = car.root.CFrame, 14
	local size = Vector3.new(car.bw * 2, car.bt, car.bl * 2) * S
	local hit = Workspace:Blockcast(cf * CFrame.new(0, car.bt / 2 * S, 0) + Vector3.new(0, RISE, 0), size, Vector3.new(0, -RISE * 2, 0), castParams)
	if hit and hit.Distance < RISE + 0.05 then car.root.CFrame = cf + Vector3.new(0, RISE + 0.05 - hit.Distance, 0) end
end
-- Studio check: how far the lowest corner of a car's outer box is above (+) or below (-) the real road parts, in metres
TERRAIN.clearance = function(car)
	local params = RaycastParams.new(); params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include; params.FilterDescendantsInstances = roadParts
	local cf, low = car.root.CFrame, math.huge
	for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, -0.5, 0, 0.5, 1 }) do
		for _, sz in ipairs({ -1, -0.75, -0.5, -0.25, 0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1 }) do
			for _, sy in ipairs({ 0, 1 }) do
				local p = cf:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(sx * car.bw, sy * car.bt, sz * car.bl) * S)
				local hit = Workspace:Raycast(p + Vector3.new(0, 200, 0), Vector3.new(0, -600, 0), params)
				if hit then low = math.min(low, (p.Y - hit.Position.Y) / S) end
			end
		end
	end
	return low
end

-- rain: an emitter that follows the camera and leans back with speed
end

local rainPart = Instance.new("Part"); rainPart.Anchored = true; rainPart.CanCollide = false; rainPart.CanQuery = false
rainPart.Transparency = 1; rainPart.Size = Vector3.new(56, 1, 48); rainPart.Parent = world
local rain = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
rain.Rate = 0; rain.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.7); rain.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Bottom
rain.Speed = NumberRange.new(150, 170); rain.Size = NumberSequence.new(0.55); rain.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.25); rain.Brightness = 3
rain.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(215, 228, 245)); rain.LightEmission = 0.7
rain.Orientation = Enum.ParticleOrientation.VelocityParallel; rain.Squash = NumberSequence.new(-3)
rain.LockedToPart = true; rain.Parent = rainPart
do -- snow for the snowy map: same emitter box as the rain, slower and softer
	local snow = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
	snow.Rate = 0; snow.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(1.6, 2.4); snow.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Bottom
	snow.Speed = NumberRange.new(26, 40); snow.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(18, 18); snow.Size = NumberSequence.new(0.22, 0.34)
	snow.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.15); snow.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(250, 252, 255)); snow.LightEmission = 0.5
	snow.Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 360); snow.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-90, 90); snow.Brightness = 2
	snow.LockedToPart = true; snow.Parent = rainPart
	TERRAIN.snow = snow
end

---------------------------------------------------------------- sound
-- rbxasset:// sounds ship with every Roblox client. Paste a Creator Store audio id (e.g. "rbxassetid://123")
-- into engine / rain / boost to add those loops; they are left empty until you pick ones you like.
local VOL, play, SOUNDS
do
local SOUND_IDS = {
	crash = "rbxasset://sounds/collide.wav",
	closeCall = "rbxasset://sounds/electronicpingshort.wav",
	tick = "rbxasset://sounds/clickfast.wav",
	pickup = "rbxasset://sounds/electronicpingshort.wav",
	land = "rbxasset://sounds/collide.wav",
	engine = "", -- looped engine sound; its pitch follows the rpm
	night = "", lofi = "", overdrive = "", -- music tracks (looped)
	rain = "",
	boost = "",
}
local SoundService = game:GetService("SoundService")
local sounds = {}
for k, id in pairs(SOUND_IDS) do
	if id ~= "" then
		local snd = Instance.new("Sound"); snd.Name = "IW_" .. k; snd.SoundId = id; snd.Volume = 0.5
		snd.Looped = (k == "engine" or k == "rain" or k == "boost" or k == "night" or k == "lofi" or k == "overdrive")
		snd.Parent = SoundService; sounds[k] = snd
	end
end
SOUNDS = sounds
VOL = { sfx = 0.8, muted = false }
function play(k, vol, pitch)
	local snd = sounds[k]; if not snd or VOL.muted then return end
	snd.Volume = (vol or 0.5) * VOL.sfx; snd.PlaybackSpeed = pitch or 1
	SoundService:PlayLocalSound(snd)
end


end

---------------------------------------------------------------- settings, progress and tuning (same values as the web version)
local GAR = {}
local SET = { steer = 1.0, autoCenter = false, rewindFx = true, motionBlur = "light", swipeSafe = false, sfx = 0.8, muted = false,
	diff = "normal", traffic = 1, weather = "clear", tod = "dusk", paint = 1, car = "vanta", mode = "endless",
	divided = true, terrain = "flat", track = "night", music = 0.7, tutorialDone = false, map = "interstate" }
local MD = {} -- oncoming traffic, Pursuit and Rush hour (filled in further down)
local PROG = { career = 0, wallet = 0, owned = {}, equip = {}, builds = {}, passes = {}, maps = {}, slots = 3, best = { endless = 0, stunt = 0, pursuit = 0, rush = 0 } }
local DIFFS = {
	easy = { traffic = 0.8, speed = 0.92, lane = 0.8, dmg = 0.14, drain = 0.65, cop = 0.92, info = "Lighter, slower traffic. Scrapes barely dent you (about 8 to wreck)." },
	normal = { traffic = 1, speed = 1, lane = 1, dmg = 0.25, drain = 1, cop = 1, info = "The standard mix. Around four side-swipes and the car is done." },
	hard = { traffic = 1.15, speed = 1.06, lane = 1.25, dmg = 0.4, drain = 1.35, cop = 1.08, info = "Denser, faster traffic with jumpier drivers. Two or three scrapes wreck you." },
}
local function curDiff() return DIFFS[SET.diff] or DIFFS.normal end
local MILE = 1609.34
local GEARS = { 3.3, 2.2, 1.6, 1.25, 1.0, 0.82, 0.68 }
local TIRE_C = 2 * math.pi * 0.35
local function finishCar(c) -- drag, gearing and the real flat-road top speed from top speed and power
	c.drag = c.power / (c.top + 3) ^ 3
	c.final = 7400 * TIRE_C / (60 * GEARS[#GEARS] * c.top)
	local v = 0
	for _ = 1, 6000 do
		local a = (math.min(1520 * (5.4 + c.power / 1.2e6), c.power / math.max(v, 3)) - c.drag * v * v - 1520 * 9.81 * 0.013) / 1520
		v = math.min(c.top, v + a * 0.02)
	end
	c.vmax = v
	return c
end
for _, c in ipairs(CARS) do finishCar(c) end
-- the car as the player has built it: base car plus equipped performance parts (or a try-on set of parts)
local function carDef(id, eq)
	local base = CARS[1]
	for _, c in ipairs(CARS) do if c.id == id then base = c end end
	return finishCar(CATALOG.tune(base, eq or CATALOG.clean(PROG, PROG.equip[base.id])))
end
local previewEq -- garage: parts being tried on before they are bought
local function lookFor(id) return previewEq or CATALOG.clean(PROG, PROG.equip[id]) end
-- TEMP: unlock every car while testing (Studio only; set to false to restore progression)
local TEST_UNLOCK_ALL = true
local function unlocked(c) if TEST_UNLOCK_ALL and game:GetService("RunService"):IsStudio() then return true end if c.robux then return PROG.passes[c.robux] == true end return c.unlock <= PROG.career end
GAR.mapOwned = function(m) if TEST_UNLOCK_ALL and game:GetService("RunService"):IsStudio() then return true end return CATALOG.mapOwned(PROG, m) end
local function paceMult(mph) return mph < 65 and 0.5 or mph < 90 and 1 or mph < 120 and 2 or mph < 160 and 3 or mph < 220 and 4 or 5 end
local function damp(a, b, k, dt) return b + (a - b) * math.exp(-k * dt) end
local function sgn(x) return x > 0 and 1 or x < 0 and -1 or 0 end
local function commas(n)
	local s = tostring(math.floor(n)); local out = s:reverse():gsub("(%d%d%d)", "%1,"):reverse()
	return (out:gsub("^,", ""))
end

---------------------------------------------------------------- server: saved settings, career points and best scores
local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local saveTimer
local function saveSettings()
	if saveTimer then task.cancel(saveTimer) end
	saveTimer = task.delay(1.5, function()
		local ev = RS:FindFirstChild("IWSave"); if ev then ev:FireServer(SET) end
	end)
end

local TRAFFIC_PAINT
---------------------------------------------------------------- game state
local keys = {}
local state = "menu" -- menu | play | over | paused
local P, playerCar, traffic = nil, nil, {}
local hist, histT, rewinding = {}, 0, nil
local camMode, camPos, shake = 0, nil, 0
local ind, indT = 0, 0
local cockpit = {}
local CUR = carDef(SET.car)
local DIFF = { traffic = 10, speed = 1, lane = 0.14 }

local function newP()
	return { x = laneX(2), v = 0, fwd = 0, f = 0, h = 0, si = 0, yaw = 0, throttle = 0, rpm = 900, gear = 1, damage = 0, score = 0,
		combo = 0, comboT = 0, bestCombo = 0, near = 0, top = 0, clock = 0, lastNearT = -9, lastNearSide = 0,
		crashed = false, crashT = 0, crashE = 0, cvz = 0, cvx = 0, spin = 0, rollV = 0, pitchV = 0, cRoll = 0, cPitch = 0, msg = "",
		busted = false, wall = false, y = 0, vy = 0, air = false, airF = 0, cleared = 0, nitro = 0.5, boosting = false, jumps = 0, longest = 0 }
end
P = newP()

-- traffic tuning follows the web version's endless curve: heavier and faster the farther you go
local function updateDiff()
	local D = curDiff()
	local amt = clamp(SET.traffic, 0, 2)
	if SET.mode == "rush" then -- dense, slow traffic
		local t = math.min(1, P.f / (6 * MILE))
		DIFF.traffic = math.floor((38 + t * 10) * D.traffic * amt + 0.5)
		DIFF.speed = (0.68 + t * 0.2) * D.speed
		DIFF.lane = math.min(0.9, (0.3 + t * 0.3) * D.lane)
	else
		local t = math.min(1, P.f / (8 * MILE))
		DIFF.traffic = math.floor((10 + t * 22) * 1.2 * D.traffic * amt + 0.5)
		DIFF.speed = (0.92 + t * 0.33) * D.speed
		DIFF.lane = math.min(0.9, (0.14 + t * 0.46) * D.lane)
	end
end

local function laneFree(lane, f, except, gap)
	for _, o in ipairs(traffic) do
		if o ~= except and (o.lane == lane or (o.change and o.change.to == lane)) and math.abs(o.f - f) < gap + (o.L + 4.6) / 2 then return false end
	end
	return true
end
-- traffic mix and paint split follow the web version
local KINDS = { { "sedan", 40 }, { "suv", 22 }, { "hatch", 12 }, { "pickup", 9 }, { "semi", 11 }, { "van", 6 } }
local TPAINT = { { Color3.fromRGB(244, 244, 241), 20 }, { Color3.fromRGB(176, 182, 188), 11 }, { Color3.fromRGB(38, 110, 214), 12 }, { Color3.fromRGB(214, 34, 40), 12 },
	{ Color3.fromRGB(240, 128, 26), 10 }, { Color3.fromRGB(244, 204, 44), 9 }, { Color3.fromRGB(28, 168, 178), 8 }, { Color3.fromRGB(70, 168, 86), 6 }, { Color3.fromRGB(150, 156, 162), 6 }, { Color3.fromRGB(122, 74, 190), 6 } }
local function weighted(list) local r = math.random() * 100; for _, e in ipairs(list) do r -= e[2]; if r <= 0 then return e[1] end end return list[1][1] end
TRAFFIC_PAINT = function() return weighted(TPAINT) end
local function laneSpeed(lane, kind)
	if kind == "semi" then return rnd(23.5, 26.5) * math.min(DIFF.speed, 1.15) end
	local r = ({ { 33, 37.5 }, { 30, 34 }, { 27, 31 }, { 24.5, 28.5 } })[lane]
	return rnd(r[1], r[2]) * DIFF.speed
end
local function spawnTraffic(f, lane)
	local kind = weighted(KINDS)
	lane = lane or math.random(1, 4)
	if kind == "semi" and lane == 1 then lane = math.random(2, 4) end
	if not laneFree(lane, f, nil, SET.mode == "rush" and 9 or 30) then return nil end
	local col = kind == "semi" and Color3.fromHSV(math.random(), 0.55, 0.7) or TRAFFIC_PAINT()
	local car = buildCar(kind, col)
	car.f = f; car.lane = lane; car.x = laneX(lane)
	car.desired = laneSpeed(lane, kind)
	car.v = car.desired; car.yawv = 0; car.side = sgn(f - P.f); car.timer = rnd(2, 6)
	traffic[#traffic + 1] = car
	return car
end
local function clearTraffic()
	for _, c in ipairs(traffic) do c.model:Destroy() end
	traffic = {}
end

			break
		end

	end
end)

---------------------------------------------------------------- UI kit (matches the web version's look)
local C_SIGN, C_DEEP, C_PAPER = Color3.fromRGB(0, 102, 63), Color3.fromRGB(0, 77, 48), Color3.fromRGB(243, 242, 236)
local C_AMBER, C_INK, C_PANEL = Color3.fromRGB(245, 181, 36), Color3.fromRGB(27, 20, 4), Color3.fromRGB(9, 13, 17)
local C_MUTED, C_BRAKE, C_SOFT = Color3.fromRGB(167, 177, 184), Color3.fromRGB(226, 54, 44), Color3.fromRGB(207, 229, 216)
local F_BLACK, F_BOLD, F_MONO = Enum.Font.GothamBlack, Enum.Font.GothamBold, Enum.Font.RobotoMono

local UI = {}
do
local function corner(o, r) local c = Instance.new("UICorner"); c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r); c.Parent = o; return c end
local function stroke(o, col, th, tr) local s = Instance.new("UIStroke"); s.Color = col; s.Thickness = th or 2; s.Transparency = tr or 0; s.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border; s.Parent = o; return s end
local function pad(o, t, r, b, l) local p = Instance.new("UIPadding"); p.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, t); p.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, r or t); p.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, b or t); p.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, l or r or t); p.Parent = o; return p end
local function vlist(o, gap, align)
	local l = Instance.new("UIListLayout"); l.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical; l.Padding = UDim.new(0, gap or 0)
	l.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder; if align then l.HorizontalAlignment = align end; l.Parent = o; return l
end
local function hlist(o, gap, valign)
	local l = Instance.new("UIListLayout"); l.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal; l.Padding = UDim.new(0, gap or 0)
	l.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder; l.VerticalAlignment = valign or Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center; l.Parent = o; return l
end
local order = 0
local function nextOrder() order += 1; return order end
local function frame(parent, props)
	local f = Instance.new("Frame"); f.BorderSizePixel = 0; f.BackgroundTransparency = 1; f.LayoutOrder = nextOrder()
	for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do f[k] = v end
	f.Parent = parent; return f
end
local function text(parent, props)
	local l = Instance.new("TextLabel"); l.BackgroundTransparency = 1; l.BorderSizePixel = 0; l.Font = F_BOLD; l.TextColor3 = C_PAPER
	l.TextSize = 14; l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; l.LayoutOrder = nextOrder()
	for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do l[k] = v end
	l.Parent = parent; return l
end
local function btn(parent, props, cb)
	local b = Instance.new("TextButton"); b.AutoButtonColor = false; b.BorderSizePixel = 0; b.Font = F_BLACK; b.TextSize = 15
	b.TextColor3 = C_PAPER; b.BackgroundTransparency = 1; b.LayoutOrder = nextOrder()
	for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do b[k] = v end
	b.Parent = parent
	if cb then b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() play("tick", 0.3, 1.3); cb() end) end
	return b
end
-- big amber / outlined buttons (".go" and ".go.alt")
local function goBtn(parent, label, alt, w, cb, dark)
	local b = btn(parent, { Size = UDim2.new(w or 1, w and 0 or 0, 0, 50), Text = string.upper(label), TextSize = 18,
		BackgroundTransparency = alt and 1 or 0, BackgroundColor3 = dark and C_INK or C_AMBER,
		TextColor3 = alt and (dark and C_INK or C_PAPER) or (dark and C_AMBER or C_INK) }, cb)
	corner(b, 8)
	if alt then stroke(b, dark and C_INK or C_PAPER, 2, dark and 0 or 0.5) end
	return b
end
-- segmented radio group (".seg")
local function seg(parent, opts, get, set)
	local row = frame(parent, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40) })
	hlist(row, 6)
	local buttons = {}
	local n = #opts
	local function refresh()
		for _, e in ipairs(buttons) do
			local on = get() == e.v
			e.b.BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 1; e.b.BackgroundColor3 = C_PAPER
			e.b.TextColor3 = on and C_DEEP or C_PAPER; e.s.Transparency = on and 0 or 0.55
		end
	end
	for _, o in ipairs(opts) do
		local b = btn(row, { Size = UDim2.new(1 / n, -6 + 6 / n, 1, 0), Text = o[2], TextSize = 15, Font = F_BLACK })
		corner(b, 7); local s = stroke(b, C_PAPER, 2, 0.55)
		local e = { b = b, s = s, v = o[1] }; buttons[#buttons + 1] = e
		b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() play("tick", 0.3, 1.3); set(o[1]); refresh() end)
	end
	refresh()
	return row, refresh
end
local function fieldLabel(parent, s, right)
	local row = frame(parent, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16) })
	text(row, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Text = string.upper(s), TextSize = 11, Font = F_BLACK, TextColor3 = C_SOFT })
	local val
	if right then val = text(row, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Text = right, TextSize = 12, Font = F_MONO, TextColor3 = C_PAPER, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right }) end
	return row, val
end
local function info(parent, s)
	return text(parent, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Text = s, TextWrapped = true, TextSize = 12, Font = F_MONO, TextColor3 = C_SOFT, LineHeight = 1.2 })
end
-- draggable slider (range input)
local activeSlider
local function slider(parent, lo, hi, step, get, set)
	local track = frame(parent, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22), BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
	local rail = frame(track, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 6), Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, -3), BackgroundTransparency = 0.82, BackgroundColor3 = C_PAPER })
	corner(rail, 3)
	local fill = frame(rail, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = C_AMBER }); corner(fill, 3)
	local knob = frame(track, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = C_PAPER })
	corner(knob, 9)
	local function refresh()
		local t = (get() - lo) / (hi - lo)
		fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(t, 1); knob.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8 + t * (rail.AbsoluteSize.X), 0.5, 0)
	end
	local function setFromX(x)
		local t = clamp((x - rail.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, rail.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 1)
		local v = lo + math.floor((t * (hi - lo)) / step + 0.5) * step
		set(clamp(v, lo, hi)); refresh()
	end
	local hit = btn(track, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Text = "" })
	hit.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function(x) activeSlider = setFromX; setFromX(x) end)
	rail:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(refresh)
	task.defer(refresh)
	return track, refresh
end
UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
	if activeSlider and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then activeSlider(i.Position.X) end
end)
UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
	if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then activeSlider = nil end
end)
local function check(parent, label, get, set)
	local b = btn(parent, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Text = "", TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left })
	local box = frame(b, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = C_PAPER })
	corner(box, 4)
	local tick = text(box, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Text = "✓", TextSize = 15, Font = F_BLACK, TextColor3 = C_DEEP, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center })
	text(b, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Text = label, TextWrapped = true, TextSize = 15, Font = F_BOLD })
	local function refresh() tick.Visible = get() and true or false end
	b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() set(not get()); refresh() end)
	refresh()
	return b, refresh
end
-- green highway-sign card (".guide") or amber warning card (".warn")
local function card(parent, w, amber)
	local outer = frame(parent, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = amber and C_AMBER or C_SIGN })
	corner(outer, 16); pad(outer, 6)
	local inner = frame(outer, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y })
	corner(inner, 11); stroke(inner, amber and C_INK or C_PAPER, 3); pad(inner, 20, 22)
	vlist(inner, 16)
	return outer, inner
end
local function overlay(name)
	local o = frame(gui, { Name = name, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 0.45, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(5, 8, 11), Visible = false, ZIndex = 5 })
	return o
end

UI.corner = corner
UI.stroke = stroke
UI.pad = pad
UI.vlist = vlist
UI.hlist = hlist
UI.frame = frame
UI.text = text
UI.btn = btn
UI.goBtn = goBtn
UI.seg = seg
UI.fieldLabel = fieldLabel
UI.info = info
UI.slider = slider
UI.check = check
UI.card = card
UI.overlay = overlay
end

-- popups (".pops": title in amber over a mono subtitle, rising and fading)
local pops = frame(hud, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.3), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(600, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y })
vlist(pops, 6, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center)
popList = {}
function popup(title, sub)
	local g = frame(pops, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(600, sub and 58 or 36) })
	local t = text(g, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34), Text = title, TextSize = 30, Font = F_BLACK, TextColor3 = C_AMBER, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, TextStrokeTransparency = 0.6 })
	local s
	if sub then s = text(g, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 36), Text = sub, TextSize = 15, Font = F_MONO, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, TextStrokeTransparency = 0.6 }) end
	popList[#popList + 1] = { g = g, t = t, s = s, age = 0 }
	while #popList > 3 do popList[1].g:Destroy(); table.remove(popList, 1) end
end
function updatePops(dt)
	for i = #popList, 1, -1 do
		local p = popList[i]; p.age += dt
		local a = p.age < 0.16 and (1 - p.age / 0.16) or (p.age > 0.98 and math.min(1, (p.age - 0.98) / 0.32) or 0)
		p.t.TextTransparency = a; if p.s then p.s.TextTransparency = a end
		p.t.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.6 + a * 0.4
		if p.age > 1.3 then p.g:Destroy(); table.remove(popList, i) end
	end
end

local gauge = frame(cluster, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1), Position = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(180, 180), BackgroundTransparency = 0.22, BackgroundColor3 = C_PANEL })
corner(gauge, 90)
local A0, SWEEP = math.pi * 0.75, math.pi * 1.5
local function polar(a, r) return UDim2.fromOffset(90 + math.cos(a) * r, 90 + math.sin(a) * r) end
G.ticks = {}; G.labels = {}; G.rpm = {}
for i = 0, 27 do -- rpm ring segments
	local a = A0 + SWEEP * (i + 0.5) / 28
	local d = frame(gauge, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = polar(a, G.R - 30), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 7), Rotation = math.deg(a) + 90, BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = C_PAPER })
	G.rpm[#G.rpm + 1] = d
end
local function buildDial(maxMph)
	for _, t in ipairs(G.ticks) do t:Destroy() end; for _, t in ipairs(G.labels) do t:Destroy() end
	G.ticks, G.labels, G.max = {}, {}, maxMph
	for m = 0, maxMph, 20 do
		local a = A0 + SWEEP * m / maxMph
		local major = m % 40 == 0
		local len = major and 12 or 7
		local col = m >= maxMph - 60 and C_BRAKE or C_PAPER
		G.ticks[#G.ticks + 1] = frame(gauge, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = polar(a, G.R - len / 2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(major and 2 or 1, len), Rotation = math.deg(a) + 90, BackgroundTransparency = 0.1, BackgroundColor3 = col })
		if major then
			G.labels[#G.labels + 1] = text(gauge, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = polar(a, G.R - 22), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 12), Text = tostring(m), TextSize = 11, Font = F_MONO, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center })
		end
	end
end
-- needle: a bar through the centre with its lower half faded out, so rotating it pivots on the hub
local needle = frame(gauge, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(90, 90), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, (G.R - 4) * 2), BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = C_BRAKE, ZIndex = 3 })
local ng = Instance.new("UIGradient"); ng.Rotation = 90
ng.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.501, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) }); ng.Parent = needle
local hub = frame(gauge, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(90, 90), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(26, 31, 36), ZIndex = 4 }); corner(hub, 7)
local gGear = text(gauge, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(90, 68), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(60, 14), Text = "D1", TextSize = 12, Font = F_MONO, TextColor3 = C_AMBER, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center })
local gSpeed = text(gauge, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(90, 126), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(100, 30), Text = "0", TextSize = 30, Font = F_BLACK, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center })
text(gauge, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(90, 146), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(60, 12), Text = "MPH", TextSize = 10, Font = F_MONO, TextColor3 = C_MUTED, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center })
function drawGauge(mph, rpm, gear)
	local maxMph = math.ceil((CUR.top * 2.23694 + 20) / 40) * 40
	if G.max ~= maxMph then buildDial(maxMph) end
	local rf = clamp(rpm / 8000, 0, 1)
	for i, d in ipairs(G.rpm) do
		local f = (i - 0.5) / #G.rpm
		if f <= rf then d.BackgroundColor3 = rf > 0.86 and C_BRAKE or C_AMBER; d.BackgroundTransparency = 0
		else d.BackgroundColor3 = f > 0.8625 and C_BRAKE or C_PAPER; d.BackgroundTransparency = f > 0.8625 and 0.45 or 0.88 end
	end
	needle.Rotation = math.deg(A0 + SWEEP * clamp(mph / maxMph, 0, 1)) + 90
	gSpeed.Text = tostring(math.floor(mph + 0.5)); gGear.Text = gear
end

-- hint box bottom-left
hint = text(hud, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 1, -16), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY,
	Text = "W/↑ gas   S/↓ brake   A D steer\nC camera   Q E indicators   R rewind 7 s   M sound   P pause", TextSize = 12, Font = F_MONO, TextColor3 = C_MUTED, BackgroundTransparency = 0.26, BackgroundColor3 = C_PANEL, LineHeight = 1.3 })
corner(hint, 6); pad(hint, 8, 10)
hint.Visible = false

-- rewind overlay
rewFx = frame(gui, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 0.82, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 90, 170), Visible = false })
local rewT = text(rewFx, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.16), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(400, 40), Text = "◀◀ REWIND", TextSize = 34, Font = F_BLACK, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(207, 232, 255), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center })
rewSec = text(rewFx, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.16, 42), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 22), Text = "-0.0 s", TextSize = 18, Font = F_MONO, TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(207, 232, 255), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center })
scan = frame(rewFx, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.14, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0.78, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(190, 225, 255) })
flash = frame(gui, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1) })
function doFlash(a) flash.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - a end

end

-- map: owned maps switch at once; locked ones are bought with points here (click twice) or with Robux in the garage shop
local fMap = field(M); local _, mapPts = fieldLabel(fMap, "Map", "")
local mapGrid = frame(fMap, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y })
local mgl = Instance.new("UIGridLayout"); mgl.CellSize = UDim2.new(1 / 3, -4, 0, 48); mgl.CellPadding = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6); mgl.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder; mgl.Parent = mapGrid
local mapBtns, mapArmed, mapArmedAt = {}, nil, 0
local function refreshMaps()
	mapPts.Text = commas(PROG.wallet) .. " pts to spend"
	for _, e in ipairs(mapBtns) do
		local on, ok = e.m.id == SET.map, GAR.mapOwned(e.m)
		e.b.BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 1; e.name.TextColor3 = on and C_DEEP or C_PAPER; e.sub.TextColor3 = on and C_DEEP or C_SOFT
		e.st.Transparency = on and 0 or 0.6
		e.sub.Text = ok and e.m.tag or ("🔒 %s pts or R$"):format(commas(e.m.price))
	end
end
for _, mp in ipairs(CATALOG.MAPS) do
	local b = btn(mapGrid, { BackgroundColor3 = C_PAPER, Text = "" })
	corner(b, 8); local st = stroke(b, C_PAPER, 2, 0.6); pad(b, 6, 9)
	local nm = text(b, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 17), Text = mp.name, TextSize = 14, Font = F_BLACK })
	local sub = text(b, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 13), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 19), Text = "", TextSize = 10, Font = F_MONO })
	mapBtns[#mapBtns + 1] = { b = b, m = mp, name = nm, sub = sub, st = st }
	b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if GAR.mapOwned(mp) then play("tick", 0.3, 1.3); SET.map = mp.id; applyTod(); refreshMaps(); saveSettings(); return end
		if PROG.wallet < mp.price then popup("LOCKED", ("Earn %s more points, or get it with Robux in Garage > Shop"):format(commas(mp.price - PROG.wallet))); return end
		if mapArmed ~= mp.id or os.clock() - mapArmedAt > 5 then
			mapArmed = mp.id; mapArmedAt = os.clock()
			popup("BUY " .. string.upper(mp.name) .. "?", ("Click it again to spend %s points"):format(commas(mp.price))); return
		end
		mapArmed = nil; GAR.buyMap(mp)
	end)
end

local function statRow(name)
	local r = frame(statsBox, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14) })
	text(r, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(82, 14), Text = name, TextSize = 11, Font = F_MONO, TextColor3 = C_SOFT })
	local bar = frame(r, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -170, 0, 7), Position = UDim2.new(0, 90, 0.5, -3), BackgroundTransparency = 0.75, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0) }); corner(bar, 3)
	local fill = frame(bar, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = C_AMBER }); corner(fill, 3)
	local val = text(r, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(70, 14), Position = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 0), Text = "", TextSize = 11, Font = F_MONO, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right })
	return { fill = fill, val = val }
end
statRows.top = statRow("TOP SPEED"); statRows.acc = statRow("POWER"); statRows.grip = statRow("GRIP"); statRows.brake = statRow("BRAKES")
local blurb = info(statsBox, ""); blurb.TextSize = 13; blurb.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
local garBtn = goBtn(fCars, "Garage: parts, builds and shop", true, nil, function() openGarage() end); garBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44); garBtn.TextSize = 15
local function refreshCars()
	careerL.Text = commas(PROG.career) .. " career pts"
	for _, e in ipairs(carBtns) do
		local on, ok = e.c.id == SET.car, unlocked(e.c)
		e.b.BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 1; e.name.TextColor3 = on and C_DEEP or C_PAPER; e.sub.TextColor3 = on and C_DEEP or C_SOFT
		e.st.Transparency = on and 0 or 0.6; e.b.Active = ok
		e.sub.Text = ok and ("%d mph"):format(math.floor(e.c.vmax * 2.23694)) or (e.c.robux and "R$ ONLY" or ("🔒 %s pts"):format(commas(e.c.unlock)))
	end
	local c = CUR
	statRows.top.fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(math.min(1, c.vmax * 2.23694 / 560), 1); statRows.top.val.Text = ("%d mph"):format(math.floor(c.vmax * 2.23694))
	statRows.acc.fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(math.min(1, c.power / 8e6), 1); statRows.acc.val.Text = ("%d hp"):format(math.floor(c.power / 745.7 / 2.2))
	statRows.grip.fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(math.min(1, c.grip / 2.2), 1); statRows.grip.val.Text = ("%.2f g"):format(c.grip)
	statRows.brake.fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(math.min(1, c.brake / 2.6), 1); statRows.brake.val.Text = ("%.2f"):format(c.brake)
	blurb.Text = c.blurb
end
for _, c in ipairs(CARS) do
	local b = btn(carGrid, { BackgroundColor3 = C_PAPER, Text = "" })
	corner(b, 8); local st = stroke(b, C_PAPER, 2, 0.6); pad(b, 6, 9)
	local nm = text(b, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 17), Text = c.name, TextSize = 14, Font = F_BLACK })
	local sub = text(b, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 13), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 19), Text = "", TextSize = 10, Font = F_MONO })
	carBtns[#carBtns + 1] = { b = b, c = c, name = nm, sub = sub, st = st }
	b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if not unlocked(c) and c.robux then popup("ROBUX ONLY", c.name); GAR.promptPass(c.robux); return end
		if not unlocked(c) then popup("LOCKED", ("Earn %s career points to unlock the %s"):format(commas(c.unlock), c.name)); return end
		play("tick", 0.3, 1.3); SET.car = c.id; CUR = c; refreshCars(); showCar(); saveSettings()
	end)
end
