	statVals[k] = text(cell, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 16), Text = "0", TextSize = 22, Font = F_MONO, TextColor3 = C_INK })
end
local oRow = frame(O, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 50) }); hlist(oRow, 10)
local againB = goBtn(oRow, "Drive again", false, 0.4, function() startGame() end, true); againB.Size = UDim2.new(0.4, -7, 0, 50)
local garB = goBtn(oRow, "Change car", true, 0.35, function() toMenu() end, true); garB.Size = UDim2.new(0.34, -7, 0, 50)
local osB = goBtn(oRow, "Settings", true, 0.26, function() openSettings() end, true); osB.Size = UDim2.new(0.26, -6, 0, 50)
local rewHint = text(O, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16), Text = "Press R to rewind 7 seconds", TextSize = 12, Font = F_MONO, TextColor3 = C_INK, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center })

---------------------------------------------------------------- pause
pauseO = overlay("Pause")
local pBox = frame(pauseO, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(460, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y })
vlist(pBox, 12, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center)
text(pBox, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 60), Text = "PAUSED", TextSize = 56, Font = F_BLACK, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center })
text(pBox, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18), Text = "Press P or Esc to resume", TextSize = 14, Font = F_MONO, TextColor3 = C_MUTED, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center })
local pRow = frame(pBox, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 50) }); hlist(pRow, 10)
goBtn(pRow, "Resume", false, 0.34, function() togglePause() end).Size = UDim2.new(1 / 3, -7, 0, 50)
goBtn(pRow, "Restart", true, 0.33, function() restartRun() end).Size = UDim2.new(1 / 3, -7, 0, 50)
goBtn(pRow, "Settings", true, 0.33, function() openSettings() end).Size = UDim2.new(1 / 3, -7, 0, 50)

---------------------------------------------------------------- settings dialog
setO = overlay("Settings"); setO.ZIndex = 8
local sCard, SC = card(setO, 480); sCard.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5); sCard.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
text(SC, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34), Text = "SETTINGS", TextSize = 30, Font = F_BLACK })
local sRef = {}
local fSteer = field(SC); local _, steerVal = fieldLabel(fSteer, "Steering sensitivity", "1.0×")
local _, rSteer = slider(fSteer, 0.5, 2.5, 0.1, function() return SET.steer end, function(v) SET.steer = v; steerVal.Text = ("%.1f×"):format(v); saveSettings() end)
local ssc = frame(fSteer, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 12) })
text(ssc, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Text = "Smooth", TextSize = 11, Font = F_MONO, TextColor3 = C_SOFT })
text(ssc, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Text = "Twitchy", TextSize = 11, Font = F_MONO, TextColor3 = C_SOFT, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right })
sRef[#sRef + 1] = function() rSteer(); steerVal.Text = ("%.1f×"):format(SET.steer) end
local _, rC = check(SC, "Auto-straighten when you let go of steering", function() return SET.autoCenter end, function(v) SET.autoCenter = v; saveSettings() end); sRef[#sRef + 1] = rC
local _, rR = check(SC, "Rewind animation (tape-style scrub back through the run)", function() return SET.rewindFx end, function(v) SET.rewindFx = v; saveSettings() end); sRef[#sRef + 1] = rR
local fBlur = field(SC); fieldLabel(fBlur, "Motion blur at speed")
local _, rB = seg(fBlur, { { "off", "Off" }, { "light", "Light" }, { "strong", "Strong" } }, function() return SET.motionBlur end, function(v) SET.motionBlur = v; saveSettings() end); sRef[#sRef + 1] = rB
local _, rS = check(SC, "Side-swipes can't wreck my car (damage stops at 99%)", function() return SET.swipeSafe end, function(v) SET.swipeSafe = v; saveSettings() end); sRef[#sRef + 1] = rS
local fTrack = field(SC); fieldLabel(fTrack, "Music")
local _, rTrack = seg(fTrack, { { "night", "Night Drive" }, { "lofi", "Lo-fi Cruise" }, { "overdrive", "Overdrive" }, { "off", "Off" } }, function() return SET.track end, function(v) SET.track = v; saveSettings() end)
sRef[#sRef + 1] = rTrack
local fMus = field(SC); local _, musVal = fieldLabel(fMus, "Music volume", "70%")
local _, rMus = slider(fMus, 0, 1, 0.01, function() return SET.music end, function(v) SET.music = v; musVal.Text = ("%d%%"):format(math.floor(v * 100 + 0.5)); saveSettings() end)
sRef[#sRef + 1] = function() rMus(); musVal.Text = ("%d%%"):format(math.floor(SET.music * 100 + 0.5)) end
local fVol = field(SC); local _, volVal = fieldLabel(fVol, "Engine & effects volume", "80%")
local _, rV = slider(fVol, 0, 1, 0.01, function() return SET.sfx end, function(v) SET.sfx = v; volVal.Text = ("%d%%"):format(math.floor(v * 100 + 0.5)); saveSettings() end)
sRef[#sRef + 1] = function() rV(); volVal.Text = ("%d%%"):format(math.floor(SET.sfx * 100 + 0.5)) end
local _, rM = check(SC, "Mute all sound  [M]", function() return SET.muted end, function(v) SET.muted = v; saveSettings() end); sRef[#sRef + 1] = rM
local wasPaused = false
goBtn(SC, "Done", false, nil, function()
	setO.Visible = false
	if wasPaused then wasPaused = false end
end)
openSettings = function()
	for _, r in ipairs(sRef) do r() end
	if state == "play" then togglePause() end
	setO.Visible = true
end

end
__build_8643()

---------------------------------------------------------------- garage: parts, saved builds and the Robux shop
local function __build_garage()
	local corner, stroke, pad, vlist, hlist, frame, text, btn, goBtn, seg = UI.corner, UI.stroke, UI.pad, UI.vlist, UI.hlist, UI.frame, UI.text, UI.btn, UI.goBtn, UI.seg
	local MarketplaceService = game:GetService("MarketplaceService")
	local PASS_PART = { paint_shift = "pnt_shift", paint_chrome = "pnt_chrome", wheels_gold = "whl_gold", pack_livery = "dec_flash", pack_neon = "lit_neon" }
	local PASS_PRICE = {}
	local tab, catSel, selPart = "parts", "engine", nil
	local O = UI.overlay("Garage"); O.BackgroundTransparency = 1; GAR.overlay = O
	local panel = frame(O, { Size = UDim2.new(0, 450, 1, -32), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16), BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = C_SIGN })
	corner(panel, 16); stroke(panel, C_PAPER, 3); pad(panel, 14); vlist(panel, 8)

	local function kfmt(n) return n >= 1000 and (tostring(math.floor(n / 1000)) .. "k") or tostring(n) end
	local function curEq() return CATALOG.clean(PROG, PROG.equip[SET.car]) end
	local function passPrice(key)
		if PASS_PRICE[key] then return "R$ " .. PASS_PRICE[key] end
		for _, p in ipairs(CATALOG.PASSES) do if p.key == key then return "R$ " .. p.rp end end
		return "R$"
	end

	-- header
	local head = frame(panel, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36) })
	text(head, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -240, 1, 0), Text = "GARAGE", TextSize = 28, Font = F_BLACK })
	local ptsL = text(head, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(140, 28), Position = UDim2.new(1, -238, 0.5, -14), BackgroundTransparency = 0.7, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), Text = "0 pts", TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Font = F_MONO, TextSize = 13 })
	corner(ptsL, 14)
	local backB = btn(head, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(90, 32), Position = UDim2.new(1, -90, 0.5, -16), Text = "BACK", TextSize = 14, BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
	corner(backB, 8); stroke(backB, C_PAPER, 2, 0.5)

	-- car picker
	local carRow = frame(panel, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40) })
	local prevB = btn(carRow, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(40, 40), Text = "<", TextSize = 20, BackgroundTransparency = 0.8, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0) }); corner(prevB, 20)
	local nextB = btn(carRow, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(40, 40), Position = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 0), Text = ">", TextSize = 20, BackgroundTransparency = 0.8, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0) }); corner(nextB, 20)
	local carName = text(carRow, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -100, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(50, 0), Text = "", TextSize = 20, Font = F_BLACK, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center })

	-- stat bars: the white bar is what you have, the amber or red tail is what the selected part would change
	local statsBox = frame(panel, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 82) }); vlist(statsBox, 6)
	local rows = {}
	for i, name in ipairs({ "TOP SPEED", "POWER", "GRIP", "BRAKES" }) do
		local r = frame(statsBox, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16) })
		text(r, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(82, 16), Text = name, TextSize = 11, Font = F_MONO, TextColor3 = C_SOFT })
		local bar = frame(r, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -200, 0, 8), Position = UDim2.new(0, 88, 0.5, -4), BackgroundTransparency = 0.75, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0) }); corner(bar, 4)
		local base = frame(bar, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = C_PAPER }); corner(base, 4)
		local delta = frame(bar, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = C_AMBER }); corner(delta, 4)
		local val = text(r, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(108, 16), Position = UDim2.new(1, -108, 0, 0), Text = "", TextSize = 11, Font = F_MONO, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, RichText = true })
		rows[i] = { base = base, delta = delta, val = val }
	end
	local function readings(c)
		return { c.vmax * 2.23694, c.power / 745.7 / 2.2, c.grip, c.brake }
	end
	local function fracs(c)
		return { c.vmax * 2.23694 / 560, math.min(1, c.power / 8e6), math.min(1, c.grip / 2.2), math.min(1, c.brake / 2.6) }
	end
	local FMT = { "%d mph", "%d hp", "%.2f g", "%.2f" }
	local DFMT = { "%+d", "%+d", "%+.2f", "%+.2f" }
	local function renderStats(candEq)
		local cur = carDef(SET.car)
		local cand = candEq and carDef(SET.car, candEq) or nil
		local a, fa = readings(cur), fracs(cur)
		local b, fb = readings(cand or cur), fracs(cand or cur)
		for i, r in ipairs(rows) do
			local lo, hi = math.min(fa[i], fb[i]), math.max(fa[i], fb[i])
			r.base.Size = UDim2.fromScale(math.min(1, lo), 1)
			r.delta.Position = UDim2.fromScale(math.min(1, lo), 0); r.delta.Size = UDim2.fromScale(math.max(0, math.min(1, hi) - math.min(1, lo)), 1)
			r.delta.BackgroundColor3 = fb[i] >= fa[i] and C_AMBER or C_BRAKE
			local v = i <= 2 and FMT[i]:format(math.floor(a[i])) or FMT[i]:format(a[i])
			local d = b[i] - a[i]
			local shown = i <= 2 and math.floor(b[i]) - math.floor(a[i]) or d
			if cand and math.abs(d) > (i <= 2 and 0.5 or 0.004) then
				v ..= (' <font color="%s">%s</font>'):format(d > 0 and "#f5b524" or "#e2362c", DFMT[i]:format(shown))
			end
			r.val.Text = v
		end
	end

	-- tabs
	local renderContent
	local _, rTab = seg(panel, { { "parts", "Parts" }, { "builds", "Builds" }, { "shop", "Shop" }, { "crates", "Crates" } }, function() return tab end, function(v) tab = v; selPart = nil; previewEq = nil; showCar(); renderContent() end)

	-- scrolling content
	local content = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame"); content.BackgroundTransparency = 1; content.BorderSizePixel = 0
	content.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -292); content.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y; content.CanvasSize = UDim2.new()
	content.ScrollBarThickness = 5; content.ScrollBarImageColor3 = C_PAPER; content.LayoutOrder = frame(nil, {}).LayoutOrder; content.Parent = panel
	local cl = vlist(content, 7); pad(content, 0, 8, 0, 0)

	-- footer
	local driveB = goBtn(panel, "Drive this build", false, nil, function() GAR.drive() end)

	---------------------------------------------------------------- server calls and state
	local function applyState(st)
		if type(st) ~= "table" then return end
		if type(st.wallet) == "number" then PROG.wallet = st.wallet end
		if type(st.career) == "number" then PROG.career = math.max(PROG.career, st.career) end
		if type(st.owned) == "table" then PROG.owned = st.owned end
		if type(st.equip) == "table" then PROG.equip = st.equip end
		if type(st.builds) == "table" then PROG.builds = st.builds end
		if type(st.passes) == "table" then PROG.passes = st.passes end
		if type(st.maps) == "table" then PROG.maps = st.maps end
		if type(st.slots) == "number" then PROG.slots = st.slots end
	end
	GAR.apply = function(st) applyState(st); CUR = carDef(SET.car) end
	local function call(action, a, b)
		local fn = RS:FindFirstChild("IWGarage")
		if not fn then return false, "The garage server is not running" end
		local ok, r1, r2, r3, r4 = pcall(function() return fn:InvokeServer(action, a, b) end)
		if not ok then return false, "Could not reach the server" end
		if type(r3) == "table" then applyState(r3) end
		return r1, r2, r3, r4
	end
	local renderAll
	GAR.sync = function()
		task.spawn(function()
			call("state")
			CUR = carDef(SET.car)
			if O.Visible then renderAll() end
		end)
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		local ev = RS:WaitForChild("IWState", 15)
		if ev then ev.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(st)
			applyState(st); CUR = carDef(SET.car)
			if O.Visible then renderAll() end
			if state == "menu" then showCar(); refreshMenu() end
		end) end
	end)
	-- live Robux prices for passes that have an id
	task.spawn(function()
		for key, id in pairs(CATALOG.PASS_IDS) do
			if id ~= 0 then
				local ok, info = pcall(function() return MarketplaceService:GetProductInfo(id, Enum.InfoType.GamePass) end)
				if ok and type(info) == "table" and info.PriceInRobux then PASS_PRICE[key] = info.PriceInRobux end
			end
		end
	end)

	---------------------------------------------------------------- actions
	local function eqWith(p) local e = curEq(); e[p.cat] = p.id; return e end
	local function equip(p)
		local e = curEq(); e[p.cat] = p.id; PROG.equip[SET.car] = e
		previewEq = nil; CUR = carDef(SET.car)
		showCar(); renderAll()
		task.spawn(function()
			local ok, msg = call("equip", SET.car, p.id)
			if not ok then popup("COULD NOT EQUIP", msg or ""); CUR = carDef(SET.car); showCar(); renderAll() end
		end)
	end
	local function promptPass(key)
		local id = CATALOG.PASS_IDS[key]
		if id ~= 0 and id then
			MarketplaceService:PromptGamePassPurchase(player, id) -- the result comes back through the IWState event
		elseif RunService:IsStudio() then
			task.spawn(function()
				local ok, msg = call("studioGrant", key)
				popup(ok and "STUDIO TEST PURCHASE" or "NOPE", msg or ""); showCar(); renderAll()
			end)
		else
			popup("COMING SOON", "This item is not on sale yet")
		end
	end
	GAR.promptPass = promptPass
	local function buy(p)
		task.spawn(function()
			local ok, msg = call("buy", p.id)
			if ok then popup("BOUGHT", p.name); task.wait(0.1); equip(p) else popup("CAN'T BUY", msg or ""); renderAll() end
		end)
	end
	local function select(p)
		selPart = p.id
		if CATALOG.owned(PROG, p) then
			if curEq()[p.cat] ~= p.id then equip(p) else previewEq = nil; renderAll() end
		else
			previewEq = eqWith(p); showCar(); renderAll()
		end
	end

	---------------------------------------------------------------- tabs
	local function clear() for _, c in ipairs(content:GetChildren()) do if not c:IsA("UIListLayout") and not c:IsA("UIPadding") then c:Destroy() end end end
	local function label(s) return text(content, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16), Text = s, TextSize = 11, Font = F_BLACK, TextColor3 = C_SOFT }) end
	local function pill(parent, props, cb) local b = btn(parent, props, cb); corner(b, 8); return b end

	local function pillFor(p)
		local eq = curEq()
		if eq[p.cat] == p.id then return "EQUIPPED", "eq" end
		if CATALOG.owned(PROG, p) then return "EQUIP", "own" end
		if p.robux then return passPrice(p.robux), "robux" end
		if p.gate and PROG.career < p.gate then return "LOCKED " .. kfmt(p.gate), "lock" end
		return commas(p.price) .. " pts", PROG.wallet >= p.price and "buy" or "poor"
	end
	local PILL = { eq = { Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.82, C_PAPER }, own = { C_PAPER, 0, C_DEEP }, buy = { C_PAPER, 0, C_DEEP }, poor = { Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.85, C_SOFT },
		robux = { C_AMBER, 0, C_INK }, lock = { Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.88, C_SOFT } }

	local function renderParts()
		local eqNow = curEq()
		-- categories: performance (points only) and look
		for _, perf in ipairs({ true, false }) do
			label(perf and "PERFORMANCE · EARNED WITH POINTS" or "LOOK")
			local grid = frame(content, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y })
			local gl = Instance.new("UIGridLayout"); gl.CellSize = UDim2.new(perf and 0.25 or 0.2, -5, 0, 30); gl.CellPadding = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 5); gl.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder; gl.Parent = grid
			for _, c in ipairs(CATALOG.CATS) do
				if (c.perf and true or false) == perf then
					local on = c.id == catSel
					local b = btn(grid, { Text = c.name, TextSize = 12, BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 1, BackgroundColor3 = C_PAPER, TextColor3 = on and C_DEEP or C_PAPER }, function()
						catSel = c.id; selPart = nil; previewEq = nil; showCar(); renderAll()
					end)
					corner(b, 15); stroke(b, C_PAPER, 1.5, on and 1 or 0.55)
				end
			end
		end
		local cat = CATALOG.cat(catSel)
		-- colour choice for decals and lights
		local eqPart = CATALOG.part(eqNow[catSel])
		local shownPart = selPart and CATALOG.part(selPart) or eqPart
		if shownPart and shownPart.colour then
			label("COLOUR")
			local sw = frame(content, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32) }); hlist(sw, 8)
			for i, col in ipairs(CATALOG.COLOURS) do
				local b = btn(sw, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 30), BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = col.c, Text = "" }, function()
					local e = curEq(); e.col = i; PROG.equip[SET.car] = e
					if previewEq then previewEq.col = i end
					showCar(); renderAll()
					task.spawn(function() call("colour", SET.car, i) end)
				end)
				corner(b, 15); stroke(b, C_PAPER, 2, eqNow.col == i and 0 or 0.8)
			end
		end
		label(string.upper(cat.name))
		local cand
		for _, p in ipairs(CATALOG.partsIn(catSel)) do
			local sel = selPart == p.id
			local row = btn(content, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 56), BackgroundTransparency = 0.72, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), Text = "" }, function() select(p) end)
			corner(row, 9); stroke(row, C_AMBER, 2, sel and 0 or 1)
			text(row, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -140, 0, 20), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 8), Text = p.name, TextSize = 15, Font = F_BLACK })
			text(row, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -140, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 30), Text = p.desc, TextSize = 11, Font = F_MONO, TextColor3 = C_SOFT })
			local label2, kind = pillFor(p)
			local st = PILL[kind]
			local pb = pill(row, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(118, 34), Position = UDim2.new(1, -126, 0.5, -17), Text = label2, TextSize = 12, BackgroundTransparency = st[2], BackgroundColor3 = st[1], TextColor3 = st[3] }, function()
				selPart = p.id
				if kind == "own" then equip(p)
				elseif kind == "buy" then buy(p)
				elseif kind == "robux" then promptPass(p.robux)
				elseif kind == "poor" then popup("NOT ENOUGH POINTS", ("Earn %s more by driving"):format(commas(p.price - PROG.wallet))); previewEq = eqWith(p); showCar(); renderAll()
				elseif kind == "lock" then popup("LOCKED", ("Reach %s career points to unlock"):format(commas(p.gate)))
				end
			end)
			if sel or (not selPart and eqNow[p.cat] == p.id) then cand = cand or (sel and eqWith(p) or nil) end
		end
		return cand
	end

	local function renderBuilds()
		label(("SAVED BUILDS · %d SLOTS"):format(PROG.slots))
		for i = 1, CATALOG.BASE_SLOTS + CATALOG.PASS_SLOTS do
			local key = tostring(i)
			local b = PROG.builds[key]
			local row = frame(content, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 70), BackgroundTransparency = 0.72, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0) }); corner(row, 9)
			if i > PROG.slots then
				text(row, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -120, 0, 20), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 12), Text = "Build " .. i .. " · locked", TextSize = 15, Font = F_BLACK, TextColor3 = C_SOFT })
				text(row, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -120, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 36), Text = "Part of the extra slots pass", TextSize = 11, Font = F_MONO, TextColor3 = C_SOFT })
				pill(row, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(90, 34), Position = UDim2.new(1, -98, 0.5, -17), Text = "SHOP", TextSize = 12, BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = C_AMBER, TextColor3 = C_INK }, function() tab = "shop"; rTab(); renderAll() end)
			else
				local title = "Build " .. i .. " · empty"
				local sub = "Saves the parts on your current car"
				if b then
					title = "Build " .. i .. " · " .. carDef(b.car).name
					local names = {}
					for _, c in ipairs(CATALOG.CATS) do
						local pid = b.eq[c.id]
						if pid and pid ~= CATALOG.DEFAULT[c.id] and CATALOG.part(pid) then names[#names + 1] = CATALOG.part(pid).name end
					end
					sub = #names > 0 and table.concat(names, " · ") or "All stock"
				end
				text(row, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -170, 0, 20), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10), Text = title, TextSize = 15, Font = F_BLACK })
				text(row, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -170, 0, 30), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 32), Text = sub, TextSize = 11, Font = F_MONO, TextColor3 = C_SOFT, TextWrapped = true, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top })
				pill(row, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(70, 28), Position = UDim2.new(1, -150, 0, 8), Text = "SAVE", TextSize = 12, BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = C_PAPER, TextColor3 = C_DEEP }, function()
					task.spawn(function() local ok, msg = call("saveBuild", i, SET.car); popup(ok and "BUILD SAVED" or "COULD NOT SAVE", ok and ("Slot " .. i) or (msg or "")); renderAll() end)
				end)
				if b then
					pill(row, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(70, 28), Position = UDim2.new(1, -76, 0, 8), Text = "LOAD", TextSize = 12, BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = C_AMBER, TextColor3 = C_INK }, function()
						task.spawn(function()
							local ok, msg, _, car = call("loadBuild", i)
							if ok and car and unlocked(carDef(car)) then SET.car = car; saveSettings() end
							CUR = carDef(SET.car); previewEq = nil; showCar(); renderAll()
							popup(ok and "BUILD LOADED" or "COULD NOT LOAD", ok and carDef(SET.car).name or (msg or ""))
						end)
					end)
					pill(row, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(70, 26), Position = UDim2.new(1, -76, 0, 40), Text = "DELETE", TextSize = 11, BackgroundTransparency = 0.8, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), TextColor3 = C_SOFT }, function()
						task.spawn(function() call("deleteBuild", i); renderAll() end)
					end)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local function renderShop()
		text(content, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Text = "Robux only buys looks, convenience and boosts. Speed and handling parts can only be earned with points, so the leaderboard stays fair.", TextWrapped = true, TextSize = 12, Font = F_MONO, TextColor3 = C_SOFT })
		for _, ps in ipairs(CATALOG.PASSES) do
			local owned = PROG.passes[ps.key] == true or (ps.kind == "map" and CATALOG.mapOwned(PROG, CATALOG.map(ps.key:sub(5))))
			local row = frame(content, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 92), BackgroundTransparency = 0.72, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0) }); corner(row, 9)
			text(row, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -140, 0, 20), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 8), Text = ps.name, TextSize = 15, Font = F_BLACK })
			text(row, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -140, 0, 12), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 28), Text = string.upper(ps.kind), TextSize = 10, Font = F_BLACK, TextColor3 = C_AMBER })
			text(row, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -140, 0, 40), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 44), Text = ps.desc, TextSize = 11, Font = F_MONO, TextColor3 = C_SOFT, TextWrapped = true, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top })
			pill(row, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(118, 34), Position = UDim2.new(1, -126, 0, 10), Text = owned and "OWNED" or passPrice(ps.key), TextSize = 12, BackgroundTransparency = owned and 0.82 or 0,
				BackgroundColor3 = owned and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or C_AMBER, TextColor3 = owned and C_PAPER or C_INK }, function() if not owned then promptPass(ps.key) end end)
			local pid = PASS_PART[ps.key]
			if pid then
				pill(row, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(118, 28), Position = UDim2.new(1, -126, 0, 52), Text = "TRY ON", TextSize = 11, BackgroundTransparency = 0.8, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), TextColor3 = C_PAPER }, function()
					local p = CATALOG.part(pid); previewEq = eqWith(p); showCar(); popup("TRY ON", p.name .. " on your " .. carDef(SET.car).name)
				end)
			end
		end
	end

	---------------------------------------------------------------- crates: bought with points, the server picks the prize, every chance is shown
	local reveal = frame(O, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 0.3, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(5, 8, 11), Visible = false, ZIndex = 20, Active = true })
	local rvCard = frame(reveal, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(540, 260), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = C_PANEL })
	corner(rvCard, 16); local rvStroke = stroke(rvCard, C_AMBER, 3)
	local CEN = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	local rvTitle = text(rvCard, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 18), Text = "", TextSize = 15, Font = F_BLACK, TextColor3 = C_SOFT, TextXAlignment = CEN })
	local rvTier = text(rvCard, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 48), Text = "", TextSize = 13, Font = F_BLACK, TextXAlignment = CEN })
	local rvName = text(rvCard, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 64), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 70), Text = "", TextSize = 26, Font = F_BLACK, TextWrapped = true, TextXAlignment = CEN })
	local rvSub = text(rvCard, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 34), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 140), Text = "", TextSize = 12, Font = F_MONO, TextColor3 = C_SOFT, TextWrapped = true, TextXAlignment = CEN })
	local rvBtns = frame(rvCard, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 44), Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 1, -62), Visible = false })
	local opening, lastCrate = false, nil
	local function openCrate(cr)
		if opening then return end
		if PROG.wallet < cr.price then popup("NOT ENOUGH POINTS", ("Earn %s more by driving"):format(commas(cr.price - PROG.wallet))); return end
		opening = true; lastCrate = cr
		reveal.Visible = true; rvBtns.Visible = false; rvStroke.Color = C_AMBER
		rvTitle.Text = string.upper(cr.name) .. "  ·  " .. commas(cr.price) .. " PTS"; rvTier.Text = ""; rvSub.Text = "Opening..."
		task.spawn(function()
			local done, ok, msg, prize = false, false, nil, nil
			task.spawn(function() local a, b, _, d = call("crate", cr.id); ok, msg, prize = a, b, d; done = true end)
			local i = 0
			while (not done or i < 26) and i < 400 do -- the reel: flick through the possible prizes, slowing down
				i += 1
				local d = cr.drops[math.random(1, #cr.drops)]
				rvName.Text = d.label; rvName.TextColor3 = CATALOG.TIERS[d.tier].c
				play("tick", 0.25, 0.9 + (i % 5) * 0.08)
				task.wait(0.045 + math.min(i, 26) * 0.007)
			end
			opening = false
			if not ok or type(prize) ~= "table" then
				reveal.Visible = false; popup("CRATE NOT OPENED", msg or "Try again"); renderAll(); return
			end
			local tier = CATALOG.TIERS[prize.tier] or CATALOG.TIERS.common
			rvTier.Text = string.upper(tier.name); rvTier.TextColor3 = tier.c; rvStroke.Color = tier.c
			rvName.Text = string.upper(tostring(prize.name or "?")); rvName.TextColor3 = tier.c
			rvSub.Text = prize.dupe and "You already own everything that slot can give, so you get points back instead."
				or (prize.kind == "points" and "Added to your points." or "It is yours now. Look for it in the garage or on the menu.")
			play("pickup", 0.8, (prize.tier == "legendary" or prize.tier == "epic") and 1.5 or 1)
			rvBtns.Visible = true
			CUR = carDef(SET.car); renderAll()
		end)
	end
	local againB = btn(rvBtns, { Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -5, 1, 0), Text = "OPEN ANOTHER", TextSize = 14, BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = C_AMBER, TextColor3 = C_INK }, function() if lastCrate then openCrate(lastCrate) end end)
	corner(againB, 8)
	local doneB = btn(rvBtns, { Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -5, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 5, 0, 0), Text = "DONE", TextSize = 14, BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, function() reveal.Visible = false end)
	corner(doneB, 8); stroke(doneB, C_PAPER, 2, 0.5)
	local function renderCrates()
		text(content, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Text = "Crates cost points, never Robux. The prize is picked by the server, and these are the exact chances.", TextWrapped = true, TextSize = 12, Font = F_MONO, TextColor3 = C_SOFT })
		for _, cr in ipairs(CATALOG.CRATES) do
			local can = PROG.wallet >= cr.price
			local row = frame(content, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 70 + #cr.drops * 17), BackgroundTransparency = 0.72, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0) }); corner(row, 9)
			text(row, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -140, 0, 20), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 8), Text = cr.name, TextSize = 15, Font = F_BLACK })
			text(row, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -140, 0, 28), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 29), Text = cr.desc, TextSize = 11, Font = F_MONO, TextColor3 = C_SOFT, TextWrapped = true, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top })
			pill(row, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(118, 34), Position = UDim2.new(1, -126, 0, 10), Text = "OPEN · " .. kfmt(cr.price), TextSize = 12, BackgroundTransparency = can and 0 or 0.85,
				BackgroundColor3 = can and C_AMBER or Color3.new(1, 1, 1), TextColor3 = can and C_INK or C_SOFT }, function() openCrate(cr) end)
			for i, d in ipairs(cr.drops) do
				local y = 62 + (i - 1) * 17
				text(row, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(40, 15), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, y), Text = d.w .. "%", TextSize = 12, Font = F_MONO, TextColor3 = CATALOG.TIERS[d.tier].c })
				text(row, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -62, 0, 15), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(52, y), Text = d.label, TextSize = 11, Font = F_MONO, TextColor3 = C_SOFT, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
			end
		end
		text(content, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Text = "Already own everything a prize could be? You get points back instead: 1,800 (Street), 7,500 (Pro) or 30,000 (Legend).", TextWrapped = true, TextSize = 11, Font = F_MONO, TextColor3 = C_SOFT })
	end

	renderContent = function()
		clear()
		local cand
		if tab == "parts" then cand = renderParts() elseif tab == "builds" then renderBuilds() elseif tab == "crates" then renderCrates() else renderShop() end
		renderStats(cand)
	end
	renderAll = function()
		ptsL.Text = commas(PROG.wallet) .. " pts"
		carName.Text = carDef(SET.car).name
		rTab()
		renderContent()
	end
	GAR.render = renderAll

	---------------------------------------------------------------- opening, closing and driving
	local function stepCar(d)
		local list = {}
		for _, c in ipairs(CARS) do if unlocked(c) then list[#list + 1] = c end end
		local idx = 1
		for i, c in ipairs(list) do if c.id == SET.car then idx = i end end
		local n = list[(idx - 1 + d) % #list + 1]
		SET.car = n.id; CUR = carDef(n.id); previewEq = nil; selPart = nil
		saveSettings(); showCar(); renderAll()
	end
	prevB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() play("tick", 0.3, 1.3); stepCar(-1) end)
	nextB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() play("tick", 0.3, 1.3); stepCar(1) end)
	openGarage = function()
		menu.Visible = false; O.Visible = true
		tab = "parts"; selPart = nil; previewEq = nil
		CUR = carDef(SET.car); showCar(); renderAll(); GAR.sync()
	end
	local function close()
		previewEq = nil; O.Visible = false; menu.Visible = true
		CUR = carDef(SET.car); showCar(); refreshMenu()
	end
	backB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() play("tick", 0.3, 1.3); close() end)
	GAR.drive = function()
		previewEq = nil; O.Visible = false
		CUR = carDef(SET.car); showCar(); startGame()
	end
	-- Studio test helpers
	GAR.open = function() openGarage() end
	GAR.cat = function(id) catSel = id; selPart = nil; previewEq = nil; showCar(); renderAll() end
	GAR.sel = function(id) local p = CATALOG.part(id); if p then catSel = p.cat; tab = "parts"; select(p) end end
	GAR.tab = function(t) tab = t; selPart = nil; previewEq = nil; showCar(); renderAll() end
	GAR.give = function() task.spawn(function() call("studioGrant", "points"); renderAll() end) end
	GAR.crate = function(id) local cr = CATALOG.crate(id); if cr then openCrate(cr) end end
	GAR.buyMap = function(mp)
		task.spawn(function()
			local ok, msg = call("buyMap", mp.id)
			if ok then popup("MAP UNLOCKED", mp.name); SET.map = mp.id; applyTod(); saveSettings() else popup("CAN'T BUY", msg or "") end
			refreshMenu()
		end)
	end

	-- the colour-shift paint cycles through every hue
	RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		local c = playerCar
		if c and c.shift and c.pp then
			local col = Color3.fromHSV((os.clock() * 0.08) % 1, 0.78, 0.92)
			for _, p in ipairs(c.pp) do p.Color = col end
		end
	end)
end
